“对不起！”普拉特带着一丝古怪的笑打断了她。“看不正的，是您自己。我拿我知道的东西，是在保护——你们全家。请您回想一下起初的话——回想我起初说的话。我说过，我发现了一桩秘密，一旦泄露，会毁掉令堂，还要连累——您！也确会如此——到了如今，更比从前厉害。所以您瞧，我保守着它，看顾的就不只是她的利益，还有——您的利益！”

===Original===
Nesta rose. She realized that there was no more to be said—or done. And Pratt rose, too, and looked at her almost appealingly.

===Chinese===
妮斯塔站起身来。她明白，再没有什么可说的——也没有什么可做的了。普拉特也站起身，几乎是恳求般地望着她。

===Original===
“I wish you’d try to see things as I’ve put them, Miss Mallathorpe,” he said. “I don’t bear malice against your mother for that scheme she contrived—I’m willing to put it clear out of my head. Why not accept things as they are? I’ll keep that secret forever—no one shall ever know about it. Why not be friends, now—why not shake hands?”

===Chinese===
“我盼望您试着按我摆出来的样子看这些事，马拉索普小姐，”他说。“她设计那桩计谋，我并不记恨令堂——我心甘情愿把它从脑子里撇个干净。何不就接受眼前的现状呢？那桩秘密，我会永远保守——永远没有人会知道。何不从此做个朋友——何不握个手？”

===Original===
He held out his hand as he spoke. But Nesta drew back.

===Chinese===
他一边说，一边伸出了手。妮斯塔却退缩了。

===Original===
“No!” she said. “My opinion is just what it was when I came here.”

===Chinese===
“不！”她说。“我的看法，同我上这儿来的时候一模一样。”

===Original===
Before Pratt could move she had turned swiftly to the door and let herself out, and in another minute she was amongst the crowds in the street below. For a few minutes she walked in the direction of Robson’s offices, but when she had nearly reached them, she turned, and went deliberately to those of Eldrick & Pascoe.

===Chinese===
不等普拉特有所动作，她已疾速转身奔向门口，自己开门出去；不出一分钟，她已置身楼下街头的人流之中。头几分钟，她朝着罗布森事务所的方向走，可眼看快要到了，她却转过身，拿定了主意似的，径直朝埃尔德里克—帕斯科的事务所去了。

## XVI. A Headquarters Conference / 十六 大本营会议

===Original===
By the time she had been admitted to Eldrick’s private room, Nesta had regained her composure; she had also had time to think, and her present action was the result of at any rate a part of her thoughts. She was calm and collected enough when she took the chair which the solicitor drew forward.

===Chinese===
等到被让进埃尔德里克的私人办公室时，妮斯塔已经恢复了镇定；她也已经有了思量的工夫，眼下这一步行动，至少是她所思所想的一部分结出的结果。律师把椅子拉上前来，她落座时，神色已是相当的平静沉着。

===Original===
“I called on you for two reasons, Mr. Eldrick,” she said. “First, to thank you for your kindness and thoughtfulness at the time of my brother’s death, in sending your clerk to help in making the arrangements.”

===Chinese===
“我这次来见您，有两层缘由，埃尔德里克先生，”她说。“第一，是谢您在我哥哥去世时的好意与体贴，承您惦念着，派您的书记员来帮忙操办各项事宜。”

===Original===
“Very glad he was of any assistance, Miss Mallathorpe,” answered Eldrick. “I thought, of course, that as he had been on the spot, as it were, when the accident happened, he could do a few little things﻿—”

===Chinese===
“他能帮上些忙，我再高兴不过了，马拉索普小姐，”埃尔德里克答道。“我当时想，出了那场意外的时候，他可说是就在出事的现场，自然能代办几件零碎小事——”

===Original===
“He was very useful in that way,” said Nesta. “And I was very much obliged to him. But the second reason for my call is﻿—I want to speak to you about him.”

===Chinese===
“那些事上他确实很得力，”妮斯塔说。“我也非常领他的情。只是我此来的第二层缘由是——我想同您谈谈他这个人。”

===Original===
“Yes?” responded Eldrick. He had already formed some idea as to what was in his visitor’s mind, and he was secretly glad of the opportunity of talking to her. “About Pratt, eh? What about him, Miss Mallathorpe?”

===Chinese===
“哦？”埃尔德里克应道。来客心里转着什么念头，他已然猜到了几分，暗自庆幸有这个机会同她谈一谈。“是关于普拉特，嗯？他怎么了，马拉索普小姐？”

===Original===
“He was with you for some years, I believe?” she asked.

===Chinese===
“他跟了您好些年，我想没说错吧？”她问。

===Original===
“A good many years,” answered Eldrick. “He came to us as office-boy, and was head-clerk when he left us.”

===Chinese===
“年头不短，”埃尔德里克答道。“他来的时候是个小听差，走的时候已经是主任书记员了。”

===Original===
“Then you ought to know him﻿—well,” she suggested.

===Chinese===
“那么您理应很——了解他，”她提示道。

===Original===
“As to that,” replied Eldrick, “there are some people in this world whom other people never could know well﻿—that’s to say, really well. I know Pratt well enough for what he was﻿—our clerk. Privately, I know little about him. He’s clever﻿—he’s ability﻿—he’s a chap who reads a good deal﻿—he’s got ambitions. And I should say he is a bit﻿—subtle.”

===Chinese===
“这可难说，”埃尔德里克答道，“这世上总有一种人，旁人是永远没法真正了解的——就是说，彻头彻尾地了解。就普拉特当我们的书记员那阵子论，我了解他算是够深的了。私下里，我对他所知甚少。他聪明——有本事——是个读书不少的家伙——有抱负。要我说，他这人还有几分——深藏不露。”

===Original===
“Deceitful?” she asked.

===Chinese===
“奸诈？”她问。

===Original===
“I couldn’t say that,” replied Eldrick. “It wouldn’t be true if I said so. I think he’s possibilities of strategy in him. But so far as we’re concerned, we found him hardworking, energetic, truthful, dependable and honest, and absolutely to be trusted in money matters. He’s had many and many a thousand pounds of ours through his hands.”

===Chinese===
“这倒不能说，”埃尔德里克答道。“我要真这么说，那就是假话了。依我看，他肚子里是有些韬略的。可就我们同他打交道的情形论，他勤勉、有干劲、诚实、可靠、品行端正，银钱事务上更是绝对信得过。经他手的我们事务所的银钱，何止成千上万镑。”

===Original===
“I believe you’re unaware that my mother, for some reason or other, unknown to me, has put him in charge of her affairs?” asked Nesta.

===Chinese===
“我想您恐怕还不知道，我母亲出于某种缘故——究竟为何连我也不知情——已经把她的全部事务交给他打理了吧？”妮斯塔问。

===Original===
“Yes﻿—Mr. Collingwood told me so,” answered Eldrick. “So, too, did your own solicitor, Mr. Robson﻿—who’s very angry about it.”

===Chinese===
“知道了——科林伍德先生告诉过我，”埃尔德里克答道。“您家自己的律师罗布森先生也说起过——他为这事恼火得很。”

===Original===
“And you?” she said, putting a direct question. “What do you think? Do please, tell me!”

===Chinese===
“那么您呢？”她单刀直入地问。“您怎么看？请千万告诉我！”

===Original===
“It’s difficult to say, Miss Mallathorpe,” replied Eldrick, with a smile and a shake of the head. “If your mother﻿—who, of course, is quite competent to decide for herself﻿—wishes to have somebody to look after her affairs, I don’t see what objection can be taken to her procedure. And if she chooses to put Linford Pratt in that position﻿—why not? As I tell you, I, as his last﻿—and only﻿—employer, am quite convinced of his abilities and probity. I suppose that as your mother’s agent, he’ll supervise her property, collect money due to her, advise her in investments, and so on. Well, I should say﻿—personally, mind﻿—he’s quite competent to do all that, and that he’ll do it honestly, I should certainly say so.”

===Chinese===
“这不好说，马拉索普小姐，”埃尔德里克答道，笑着摇了摇头。“倘若令堂——她当然完全有资格给自己拿主意——想找个人照管她的事务，我看不出她这套做法有什么可指摘的。她既然愿意让林福德·普拉特坐这个位子——又有什么不可以？正如我跟您说的，我作为他最后一位——也是唯一一位——雇主，对他的才干与操守深信不疑。我料想，他以令堂代理人的身份，会监管她的产业、代收她名下的进项、在投资上给她出主意，如此等等。那么，要我说——请注意，纯属个人之见——这些事他样样办得了，而且会办得清清白白，这一点我敢打包票。”

===Original===
“But﻿—why should he do it at all?” asked Nesta.

===Chinese===
“可是——他到底为什么要揽这桩差事？”妮斯塔问。

===Original===
Eldrick waved his hands.

===Chinese===
埃尔德里克摆了摆手。

===Original===
“Ah!” he exclaimed. “Now you ask me a very different question! But﻿—I understand﻿—in fact, I know﻿—that Pratt turns out to be a relation of yours﻿—distant, but it’s there. Perhaps your mother﻿—who, of course, is much better off since your brother’s sad death﻿—is desirous of benefiting Pratt﻿—as a relation.”

===Chinese===
“啊！”他叫道。“这您可问了个全然不同的问题了！不过——我了解到——实际上，我是确切知道——普拉特原来竟是你们家的一门亲戚——远是远了些，可这层关系确凿是在的。或许令堂——令兄惨遭不幸之后，她的境况自然宽裕多了——是有意照拂普拉特——念的是亲戚情分。”

===Original===
“Do you advise anything?” asked Nesta.

===Chinese===
“您有什么指教么？”妮斯塔问。

===Original===
“Well, you know, Miss Mallathorpe,” replied Eldrick, smiling. “I’m not your legal adviser. What about Mr. Robson?”

===Chinese===
“呃，这个，您知道的，马拉索普小姐，”埃尔德里克笑吟吟地答道。“我可不是您的法律顾问。罗布森先生怎么说？”

===Original===
“Mr. Robson is so very angry about all this﻿—with my mother,” said Nesta, “that I don’t even want to ask his advice. What I really do want is the advice, counsel, of somebody﻿—perhaps more as a friend than as a solicitor.”

===Chinese===
“罗布森先生为这一切——冲着我母亲——火气大得很，”妮斯塔说，“闹得我连他的主意都不想去讨了。我真正想要的，是有人给我出出主意、指条明路——与其把人家当律师，不如把人家当朋友。”

===Original===
“Delighted to give you any help I can﻿—either professionally or as a friend,” exclaimed Eldrick. “But﻿—let me suggest something. And first of all﻿—is there anything﻿—something﻿—in all this that you haven’t told to anybody yet?”

===Chinese===
“无论以律师的身份还是以朋友的身份，但凡帮得上忙，我都乐意之至，”埃尔德里克朗声道。“不过——且容我提个建议。首先——这一切里头，是不是有什么——某桩事——您至今还没对任何人讲过？”

===Original===
“Yes﻿—much!” she answered. “A great deal!”

===Chinese===
“有——多着呢！”她答道。“多得很！”

===Original===
“Then,” said Eldrick, “let me advise a certain counsel. Two heads are better than one. Let me ask Mr. Collingwood to come here.”

===Chinese===
“那么，”埃尔德里克说，“且容我献上一策。两个人的脑子，总强过一个人的。我这就请科林伍德先生到这儿来。”

===Original===
He was watching his visitor narrowly as he said this, and he saw a faint rise of colour in her cheeks. But for the moment she did not answer, and Eldrick saw that she was thinking.

===Chinese===
说这话时，他细细察看着来客的神色，看见她两颊上微微泛起一层红晕。她一时没有作声，埃尔德里克看得出，她在掂量。

===Original===
“I can get him across from his chambers in a few minutes,” he said. “He’s sure to be in just now.”

===Chinese===
“我几分钟就能把他从他的事务室里请过来，”他说。“这会儿他准在。”

===Original===
“Can I have a few minutes to decide?” asked Nesta.

===Chinese===
“能容我几分钟拿定主意么？”妮斯塔问。

===Original===
Eldrick jumped up.

===Chinese===
埃尔德里克腾地站起身来。

===Original===
“Of course!” he said. “I’ll leave you a while. It so happens I want to see my partner, I’ll go up to his room, and return to you presently.”

===Chinese===
“当然可以！”他说。“我暂且告退片刻。恰好我要见见我的合伙人，我上他屋里去一趟，回头就来。”

===Original===
Nesta, left alone, gave herself up to deep thought, and to a careful reckoning of her position. She was longing to confide in some trustworthy person or persons, for Pratt’s revelations had plunged her into a maze of perplexity. But her difficulties were many. First of all, she would have to tell all about the terrible charge brought by Pratt against her mother. Then about the second which he professed to﻿—or probably did﻿—hold. What sort of a secret could it be? And supposing her advisers suggested strong measures against Pratt﻿—what then, about the danger to her mother, in a twofold direction?

===Chinese===
妮斯塔独自留下，沉入了深深的思索，细细掂量着自己的处境。她渴望向某个信得过的人倾诉——普拉特那一番话，已把她推进了重重的疑阵。可她的难处也实在多。头一件，她得把普拉特扣在她母亲头上的那桩可怕指控，原原本本讲出来。其次，还有他自称握有的——或者多半确实握有的——第二重把柄。那能是桩什么样的秘密呢？再者，倘若出主意的人建议对普拉特采取强硬手段——那她母亲两头招着的祸患，又当如何是好？

===Original===
Would it be better, wiser, if she kept all this to herself at present, and waited for events to develop? But at the mere thought of that, she shrank, feeling mentally and physically afraid﻿—to keep all that knowledge to herself, to brood over it in secret, to wonder what it all meant, what lay beneath, what might develop, that was more than she felt able to bear. And when Eldrick came back she looked at him and nodded.

===Chinese===
眼下若把这一切闷在自己肚里，静候事态自行发展，会不会更妥当、更高明些？可单是这么一想，她便先怯了，身子上、心里都发怵——把那一肚子知晓的事独个儿守着，暗地里翻来覆去地熬煎，揣度这一切到底意味着什么、底下埋着什么、还会生出什么枝节，这实在超出了她自觉承受得起的限度。待埃尔德里克回来，她望着他，点了点头。

===Original===
“I should like to talk to you and Mr. Collingwood,” she said quietly.

===Chinese===
“我愿意同您和科林伍德先生谈一谈，”她轻声说。

===Original===
Collingwood came across to Eldrick’s office at once. And to these two Nesta unbosomed herself of every detail that she could remember of her interview with Pratt﻿—and as she went on, from one thing to another, she saw the men’s faces grow graver and graver, and realized that this was a more anxious matter than she had thought.

===Chinese===
科林伍德当即从对面赶到埃尔德里克的办公室。妮斯塔对着他们两位，把同普拉特那番对答中记得起的每一个细节，都倾囊倒出——她一桩接一桩讲下去，眼见两位先生的脸色越听越凝重，这才明白，此事比她原先想的还要棘手。

===Original===
“That’s all,” she said in the end. “I don’t think I’ve forgotten anything. And even now, I don’t know if I’ve done right to tell you all this. But﻿—I don’t think I could have faced it﻿—alone!”

===Chinese===
“要说的就这些了，”她末了说。“我想我没落下什么。直到这会儿，我也不知道把这些统统告诉你们，到底对不对。可是——这桩事若叫我一个人扛着——我怕是撑不住的！”

===Original===
“My dear Miss Mallathorpe!” said Eldrick earnestly. “You’ve done the wisest thing you probably ever did in your life! Now,” he went on, looking at Collingwood, “just let us all three realize what is to me a more important fact. Nobody would be more astonished than Pratt to know that you have taken the wise step you have. You agree, Collingwood?”

===Chinese===
“我亲爱的马拉索普小姐！”埃尔德里克恳切地说。“您这是做了一件您这辈子大概顶顶明智的事！”他接着望向科林伍德说：“现在，咱们仨都得认清一个在我看来更要紧的事实。普拉特若知道您竟走了这样一步明智的棋，天下没有谁比他更吃惊。你同意吧，科林伍德？”

===Original===
“Yes!” answered Collingwood, after a moment’s reflection. “I think so.”

===Chinese===
“同意！”科林伍德沉吟片刻，答道。“我想是这样。”

===Original===
“Miss Mallathorpe doesn’t quite see what we mean,” said Eldrick, turning to Nesta. “We mean that Pratt firmly believed, when he told you what he did, that for your mother’s sake and your own, you would keep his communication a dead secret. He firmly believed that you would never dare to tell anybody what he told you. Most people﻿—in your position﻿—wouldn’t have told. They’d have let the secret eat their lives out. You’re a wise and a sensible young woman! And the thing is﻿—we must let Pratt remain under the impression that you are keeping your knowledge to yourself. Let him continue to believe that you’ll remain silent under fear. And let us meet his secret policy with a secret strategy of our own!”

===Chinese===
“马拉索普小姐还不大明白我们的意思，”埃尔德里克转向妮斯塔说。“我们的意思是：普拉特对您说那些话的时候，笃信不疑：为了令堂的缘故，也为了您自己的缘故，您会把他的话守成一个死秘。他笃信您绝不敢把他讲的话告诉任何人。大多数人——处在您的境地——都不会说出去。只会由着那桩秘密把一辈子蚕食殆尽。您是位有头脑、明事理的姑娘！而现在要紧的是——咱们得让普拉特继续以为，您把知道的一切都闷在了自己心里。让他接着相信，您慑于恐惧，只会一声不吭。而咱们呢，就拿自家的秘密战略，去对付他的秘密策略！”

===Original===
Again he glanced at Collingwood, and again Collingwood nodded assent.

===Chinese===
他又瞥了科林伍德一眼，科林伍德也再次点头称是。

===Original===
“Now,” continued Eldrick, “just let us consider matters for a few minutes from the position which has newly arisen. To begin with. Pratt’s account of your mother’s dealings about the footbridge is a very clever and plausible one. I can see quite well that it has caused you great pain; so before I go any further, just let me say this to you﻿—don’t you attach one word of importance to it!”

===Chinese===
“现在，”埃尔德里克接着说，“且让咱们就眼前的新局面，把事情掂量几分钟。头一件。普拉特讲的令堂在便桥上头的那套作为，编得很巧，也很有几分可信。我完全看得出来，它让您痛苦万分；所以在往下谈之前，且容我对您说这么一句——它一个字您也别当真！”

===Original===
Nesta uttered a heartfelt cry of relief.

===Chinese===
妮斯塔由衷地迸出一声如释重负的呼喊。

===Original===
“Oh!” she exclaimed. “If you knew how thankful I should be to know that it’s all lies﻿—that he was lying! Can I really think that﻿—after what I saw?”

===Chinese===
“哦！”她叫道。“您哪里知道，若能确知那全是谎话——是他信口撒谎——我会多么感激！可我当真能这么想么——在亲眼见识过那些之后？”

===Original===
“I won’t ask you to think that he’s telling lies﻿—just now,” answered Eldrick, with a glance at Collingwood, “but I’ll ask you to believe that your mother could put a totally different aspect and complexion on all her actions and words in connection with the entire affair. My impression, of course,” he went on, with something very like a wink at Collingwood, “is that Mrs. Mallathorpe, when she wrote that letter to Pratt, intended to have the bridge mended first thing next morning, and that something prevented that being done, and that when she was seen about the shrubberies in the afternoon, she was on her way to meet Pratt before he could reach the dangerous point, so that she could warn him. What do you say, Collingwood?”

===Chinese===
“我还不至于要您认定他在撒谎——眼下还不到时候，”埃尔德里克答道，朝科林伍德瞥了一眼，“但我要请您相信：对整桩事情里令堂的一言一行，她完全可以给出一个截然不同的面貌和说法。”他继续往下说，那副神情，几乎就是朝科林伍德使了个眼色。“依我的看法嘛，马拉索普太太给普拉特写那封信的时候，原打算第二天一早头一件事就是叫人把桥修好，只是有什么事绊住，没能办成；而下午她叫人瞧见在灌木林一带转悠，那是因为她正赶在前头去迎普拉特，要在他走到危险地段之前提醒他。你说呢，科林伍德？”

===Original===
“I should say,” answered Collingwood, regarding the solicitor earnestly, and speaking with great gravity of manner, “that that would make an admirable line of defence to any charge which Pratt was wicked enough to prefer.”

===Chinese===
“我要说，”科林伍德答道，一脸郑重地望着这位律师，语气也极凝重，“普拉特倘若当真歹毒到提出什么指控，这套说法都足以构成一条漂亮的辩护路线。”

===Original===
“You don’t think my mother meant﻿—meant to﻿—” exclaimed Nesta, eagerly turning from one man to the other. “You﻿—don’t?”

===Chinese===
“你们并不认为我母亲存心——存心要——”妮斯塔叫道，急切地把目光从一个人脸上移到另一个人脸上。“你们——不认为？”

===Original===
“There is no evidence worth twopence against your mother!” replied Eldrick soothingly. “Put everything that Pratt has said against her clear out of your mind. Put all recent events out of your mind! Don’t interfere with Pratt﻿—just now. The thing to be done about Pratt is this﻿—and it’s the only thing. We must find out﻿—exactly, as secretly as possible﻿—what this secret is of which he speaks. What is this hold on Mrs. Mallathorpe? What is this document to which he refers? In other words, we must work back to some point which at present we can’t see. At least, I can’t see it. But﻿—we may discover it. What do you say, Collingwood?”

===Chinese===
“指证令堂的证据，连两个便士都不值！”埃尔德里克宽慰道。“把普拉特说的那套指控她的话，从您脑子里统统撇出去。把近日种种也统统撇出去！普拉特那边，眼下先别去动他。对付普拉特要办的是这件事——也是唯一的一件。咱们必须查个水落石出——查得确切，又尽可能地隐秘——他嘴里这桩秘密究竟是什么。他攥着马拉索普太太的把柄究竟是什么？他指的那份文书又是什么？换句话说，咱们必须往回追，追到某个咱们眼下还看不见的节点上。至少，我是看不见。不过——未必就查不出来。你说呢，科林伍德？”

===Original===
“I agree entirely,” answered Collingwood. “Let Pratt rest in his fancied security. The thing is, certainly, to go back. But﻿—to what point?”

===Chinese===
“我完全同意，”科林伍德答道。“就让普拉特在他自以为安稳的错觉里待着。要紧的，自然是往回追查。可是——追到哪个节点呢？”

===Original===
“That we must consider later,” said Eldrick. “Now﻿—for the present, Miss Mallathorpe﻿—you are, I suppose, going back home?”

===Chinese===
“那个咱们回头再议，”埃尔德里克说。“眼下——马拉索普小姐——您这就回家了，我想是吧？”

===Original===
“Yes, at once,” answered Nesta. “I have my car at the Crown Hotel.”

===Chinese===
“是的，马上就走，”妮斯塔答道。“我的车寄在克朗旅馆（Crown Hotel）。”

===Original===
“I should just like to know something,” continued Eldrick again, looking at Collingwood as if for approval. “That is﻿—Mrs. Mallathorpe’s present disposition towards affairs in general and Pratt in particular. Miss Mallathorpe!﻿—just do something which I will now suggest to you. When you reach home, see your mother﻿—she is still, I understand, an invalid, though evidently able to transact business. Just approach her gently and kindly, and tell her that you are a little﻿—should we say uncomfortable?﻿—about certain business arrangements which you hear she has made with Mr. Pratt, and ask her, if she won’t talk them over with you, and give you her full confidence. It’s now half-past twelve,” continued Eldrick, looking at his watch. “You’ll be home before lunch. See your mother early in the afternoon, and then telephone, briefly, the result to me, here, at four o’clock. Then﻿—Mr. Collingwood and I will have a consultation.”

===Chinese===
“我还想弄清楚一件事，”埃尔德里克又接下去说，一面望着科林伍德，仿佛在讨他的首肯。“那就是——马拉索普太太如今对种种事务、尤其对普拉特本人的心意。马拉索普小姐！——请您照我下面建议的去做一件事。您到家之后，去见令堂——据我所知，她眼下还带着病身子，不过办起事务来显然无碍。您只管温温和和、怜惜体贴地凑上前去，告诉她，您听说她同普拉特先生做了几桩业务上的安排，心里有些个——咱们不妨说，隐隐不安？——并且求她把这些事同您细细商量，对您推心置腹。”埃尔德里克看了看表，接着说。“现在是十二点半。您午饭前就能到家。下午一早去见令堂，四点钟把结果简要地打电话告诉我——打到这儿来。然后——我和科林伍德先生再会商一番。”

===Original===
He motioned Collingwood to remain where he was, and himself saw Nesta down to the street. When he came back to his room he shook his head at the young barrister.

===Chinese===
他示意科林伍德留在原地，自己把妮斯塔送到街上。回到房里，他冲这位年轻出庭律师摇了摇头。

===Original===
“Collingwood!” he said. “There’s some dreadful business afloat in all this! And it’s all the worse because of the fashion in which Pratt talked to that girl. She’s evidently a very good memory﻿—she narrated that conversation clearly and fully. Pratt must be very sure of his hand if he showed her his cards in that way﻿—his very confidence in himself shows what a subtle network he’s either made or is making. I question if he’d very much care if he knew that we know. But he mustn’t know that﻿—yet. We must reply to his mine with a countermine!”

===Chinese===
“科林伍德！”他说。“这整桩事底下，正在酝酿一桩骇人的勾当！而普拉特对那姑娘讲话的那副做派，更叫事情糟上加糟。她显然记性极好——那番对答她复述得清清楚楚、一点不落。普拉特敢那样朝她摊牌，必是对自己手里的牌十拿九稳——他这份自信本身，恰恰显出他已经织成、或者正在织一张何等绵密的罗网。就算他知道咱们已经知道了，我看他也未必怎么放在心上。可这个，眼下还不能让他知道。他掘坑道，咱们便对挖一条反坑道！”

===Original===
“What do you think of Pratt’s charge against Mrs. Mallathorpe?” asked Collingwood.

===Chinese===
“普拉特对马拉索普太太的那番指控，您怎么看？”科林伍德问。

===Original===
Eldrick made a wry face.

===Chinese===
埃尔德里克做了个苦脸。

===Original===
“Looks bad!﻿—very, very bad, Collingwood!” he answered. “Art and scheme of a desperate woman, of course. But﻿—we mustn’t let her daughter think we believe it. Let her stick to the suggestion I made﻿—which, as you remarked, would certainly make a very good line of defence, supposing Pratt even did accuse her. But now﻿—what on earth is this document that’s been mentioned﻿—this paper of which Pratt has possession? Has Mrs. Mallathorpe at some time committed forgery﻿—or bigamy﻿—or﻿—what is it? One thing’s sure, however﻿—we’ve got to work quietly. We mustn’t let Pratt know that we’re working. I hope he doesn’t know that Miss Mallathorpe came here. Will you come back about four and hear what message she sends me? After that, we could consult.”

===Chinese===
“难看！——非常非常难看，科林伍德！”他答道。“自然喽，那是一个铤而走险的女人的伎俩与图谋。可是——咱们决不能让她的女儿以为咱们信了它。让她咬住我提的那套说法——正如你方才说的，哪怕普拉特果真去控告她，那也定能构成一条极好的辩护路线。可是现在——他们提到的那份文书究竟是什么东西——普拉特攥在手里的那张纸，到底是什么？马拉索普太太莫非在什么时候犯过伪造罪——或是重婚罪——还是——究竟是什么？不过有一点是定了的——咱们只能悄悄地干。决不能让普拉特知道咱们在查。但愿他不知道马拉索普小姐来过这儿。四点钟你回来，听听她给我捎来什么口信，好不好？之后，咱们再行会商。”

===Original===
Collingwood went away to his chambers. He was much occupied just then, and had little time to think of anything but the work in hand. But as he ate his lunch at the club which he had joined on settling in Barford, he tried to get at some notion of the state of things, and once more his mind reverted to the time of his grandfather’s death, and his own suspicions about Pratt at that period. Clearly that was a point to which they must hark back﻿—he himself must make more inquiries about the circumstances of Antony Bartle’s last hours. For this affair would not have to rest where it was﻿—it was intolerable that Nesta Mallathorpe should in any way be under Pratt’s power. He went back to Eldrick at four o’clock with a suggestion or two in his mind. And at the sight of him Eldrick shook his head.

===Chinese===
科林伍德回自己的事务室去了。他那时手头正忙，除了眼前的活计，几乎无暇他顾。可当他在自己落脚巴福德后加入的那家俱乐部里用午饭时，还是试着把眼下的局面理出个头绪；他的心思又一次回到祖父去世的那段时日，回到他当年对普拉特起的那些疑心。显然，那正是他们必须回溯的一个节点——他本人得再去打听打听安东尼·巴特尔临终前最后几个钟头的种种情形。因为这桩事断不能就停在此处——妮斯塔·马拉索普竟要被普拉特攥在掌心里听凭摆布，这是断断容不下的。四点钟，他带着一两条腹案回到埃尔德里克那里。而他一露面，埃尔德里克就冲他摇头。

===Original===
“I’ve had that telephone message from Normandale,” he said, “five minutes ago. Pretty much what I expected﻿—at this juncture, anyway. Mrs. Mallathorpe absolutely declines to talk business with even her daughter at present﻿—and earnestly desires that Mr. Linford Pratt may be left alone.”

===Chinese===
“诺曼代尔来的电话，我接到了，”他说，“五分钟前。同我料想的不相上下——至少眼下这个关口是如此。马拉索普太太此刻断然拒绝同任何人谈事务——连亲女儿也不例外——并且恳切希望，切莫再去惊动林福德·普拉特先生。”

===Original===
“Well?” asked Collingwood after a pause. “What now?”

===Chinese===
“哦？”科林伍德顿了顿，问道。“如今怎么办？”

===Original===
“We must do what we can﻿—secretly, privately, for the daughter’s sake,” said Eldrick. “I confess I don’t quite see a beginning, but﻿—”

===Chinese===
“咱们必须尽力而为——悄悄地、私底下，为了那姑娘的缘故，”埃尔德里克说。“我承认，我还不大看得出该从哪里入手，不过——”

===Original===
Just then the private door opened, and Pascoe, a somewhat lackadaisical-mannered man, who always looked half-asleep, and was in reality remarkably wide-awake, lounged in, nodded to Collingwood, and threw a newspaper in front of his partner.

===Chinese===
正当这时，那扇内间的门开了，帕斯科踱了进来。此君举止慢条斯理，永远一副睡不醒的样子，实则警醒得了不得。他朝科林伍德点了点头，把一份报纸丢在合伙人面前。

===Original===
“I say, Eldrick,” he drawled, as he removed a newly-lighted cigar from his lips. “There’s an advertisement here which seems to refer to that precious protégé of yours, who left you with such scant ceremony. Same name, anyhow!”

===Chinese===
“我说，埃尔德里克，”他把嘴里刚点着的雪茄挪开，慢吞吞地说。“这儿有一则广告，瞧着像是冲你那位宝贝门生去的——就是那个连半点礼数都不讲就把你甩下的人。反正，名字是一模一样！”

===Original===
Eldrick snatched up the paper, glanced at it and read a few words aloud.

===Chinese===
埃尔德里克一把抓起报纸，扫了一眼，高声念出几个字。

===Original===
**Information wanted** about James Parrawhite, at one time in practice as a solicitor.

===Chinese===
**征集消息**：詹姆斯·帕拉怀特，曾一度开业执业为律师。

## XVII. Advertisement / 十七 广告

===Original===
Eldrick looked up at his partner with a sharp, confirmatory glance.

===Chinese===
埃尔德里克抬起头，朝合伙人投去锐利而求证的一瞥。

===Original===
“That’s our Parrawhite, of course!” he said. “Who’s after him, now?” And he went on to read the rest of the advertisement, murmuring its phraseology half-aloud: “ ‘in practice as a solicitor at Nottingham and who left that town six years ago. If the said James Parrawhite will communicate with the undersigned he will hear something greatly to his advantage. Any person able to give information as to his whereabouts will be suitably rewarded. Apply to Halstead & Byner, 56B, St. Martin’s Chambers, London, W.C.’ Um!﻿—Pascoe, hand over that Law List.”

===Chinese===
“这不正是咱们那位帕拉怀特嘛！”他说。“这会儿又是谁在找他？”他接着把广告的其余部分念完，半高声地咀嚼着其中的措辞：“‘曾在诺丁汉（Nottingham）开业执业为律师，并于六年前离开该城。如该詹姆斯·帕拉怀特本人与署名人联系，将闻悉于其大有利益之事。凡能提供其行踪消息者，必获相称酬报。请洽哈尔斯特德—拜纳事务所（Halstead & Byner），圣马丁事务室56B号，伦敦中西区。’嗯！——帕斯科，把那本《律师名录》（Law List）递来。”

===Original===
Collingwood looked on in silence while Eldrick turned over the pages of the big book which his partner took down from a shelf. He wondered at Eldrick’s apparent and almost eager interest.

===Chinese===
合伙人从架上取下那本大部头，埃尔德里克一页页翻检着，科林伍德在一旁默默看着。埃尔德里克脸上那份近乎急切的兴致，令他暗自纳罕。

===Original===
“Halstead & Byner are not solicitors,” announced Eldrick presently. “They must be inquiry agents or something of that sort. Anyway, I’ll write to them, Pascoe, at once.”

===Chinese===
“哈尔斯特德—拜纳不是律师，”埃尔德里克过了一会儿宣布道。“他们准是征信代理人，要么就是那一类的行当。不管怎样，帕斯科，我这就给他们写信。”

===Original===
“You don’t know where the fellow is,” said Pascoe. “What’s the good?”

===Chinese===
“可你不知道那家伙如今在哪儿，”帕斯科说。“写了又有什么用？”

===Original===
“No﻿—but we know where he last was,” retorted Eldrick. He turned to Collingwood as the junior partner sauntered out of the room. “Rather odd that Pascoe should draw my attention to that just now,” he remarked. “This man Parrawhite was, in a certain sense, mixed up with Pratt﻿—at least, Pratt and I are the only two people who know the secret of Parrawhite’s disappearance from these offices. That was just about the time of your grandfather’s death.”

===Chinese===
“是不知道——可我们知道他最后待过的地方，”埃尔德里克反诘道。初级合伙人慢悠悠踱出房去，他便转向科林伍德。“帕斯科偏偏这会儿引我去看那则广告，倒是相当蹊跷，”他说。“这个帕拉怀特，在某种意义上，是同普拉特搅在一处的——至少，帕拉怀特从这几间办公室里失踪的秘密，天底下只有普拉特同我两个人知道。那正是您祖父去世前后的事。”

===Original===
Collingwood immediately became attentive. His first suspicions of Pratt were formed at the time of which Eldrick spoke, and any reference to events contemporary excited his interest.

===Chinese===
科林伍德顿时留上了心。他对普拉特最初的疑心，正是在埃尔德里克所说的那段时日里生出来的，凡提到那时候的事，都吊得起他的兴致。

===Original===
“Who was or is﻿—this man you’re talking of?” he asked.

===Chinese===
“这个人——您说的这位——究竟是什么路数？”他问。

===Original===
“Bad lot﻿—very!” answered Eldrick, shaking his head. “He and I were articled together, at the same time, to the same people: we saw a lot of each other as fellow articled clerks. He afterwards practised in Nottingham, and he held some good appointments. But he’d a perfect mania for gambling﻿—the turf﻿—and he went utterly wrong, and misappropriated clients’ money, and in the end he got into prison, and was, of course, struck off the rolls. I never heard anything of him for years, and then one day, some time ago, he turned up here and begged me to give him a job. I did﻿—and I’ll do him the credit to say that he earned his money. But﻿—in the end, his natural badness broke out. One afternoon﻿—I’m careless about some things﻿—I left some money lying in this drawer﻿—about forty pounds in notes and gold﻿—and next morning Parrawhite never came to business. We’ve never seen or heard of him since.”

===Chinese===
“坏坯子——彻头彻尾！”埃尔德里克摇着头答道。“他跟我同年订下学徒契约，拜在同一拨人门下：同做见习书记员那阵子，我俩来往得多。他后来在诺丁汉开业执业，也谋到过几个体面的职位。可他嗜赌成癖——专在赛马上下注——就此彻底毁了道行，挪用当事人的钱款，末了锒铛入狱，自然也被从律师名册上除了名。一连多年我没听到他半点音讯，后来有一天——也就是前些时候——他忽然跑到这儿，求我赏他个差事。我给了——凭良心说句公道话，那份工钱他是挣得出来的。可是——到头来，他骨子里的劣根性还是冒了头。有一天下午——我在有些事上素来马虎——我把一笔钱落在了这个抽屉里——纸币加金币约有四十镑——第二天早上，帕拉怀特就没再来上工。打那以后，我们再没见过他，也没再听到他半点音讯。”

===Original===
“You mentioned Pratt,” said Collingwood.

===Chinese===
“您方才提到了普拉特，”科林伍德说。

===Original===
“Only Pratt and I know﻿—about the money,” replied Eldrick. “We kept it secret﻿—I didn’t want Pascoe to know I’d been so careless. Pascoe didn’t like Parrawhite﻿—and he doesn’t know his record. I only told him that Parrawhite was a chap I’d known in better circumstances and wanted to give a hand to.”

===Chinese===
“那笔钱的事，只有普拉特同我知道，”埃尔德里克答道。“我们把它瞒了下来——我不想让帕斯科知道我竟那么粗心。帕斯科不喜欢帕拉怀特——他也不知道此人的底细。我只跟他说，帕拉怀特是我境遇好时结识的一个人，想拉他一把。”

===Original===
“You said it was about the time of my grandfather’s death?” asked Collingwood.

===Chinese===
“您说那正是我祖父去世的那段时日？”科林伍德问。

===Original===
“It was just about then﻿—between his death and his funeral I should say,” answered Eldrick. “The two events are associated in my mind. Anyway, I’d like to know what it is that these people want Parrawhite for. If it’s money that’s come to him, it’ll be of no advantage﻿—it’ll only go where all the rest’s gone.”

===Chinese===
“正是那前后——我估摸着，就在他咽气到下葬之间，”埃尔德里克答道。“这两桩事在我脑子里是拴在一处的。不管怎样，我倒想知道，这些人找帕拉怀特究竟为的什么。若是天上掉下来一笔钱到了他头上，那也于他无益——只会跟着先前那些钱财去同一个去处。”

===Original===
Collingwood lost interest in Parrawhite. Parrawhite appeared to have nothing to do with the affairs in which he was interested. He sat down and began to tell Eldrick about his own suspicions of Pratt at the time of Antony Bartle’s death; of what Jabey Naylor had told him about the paper taken from the *History of Barford*; of the lad’s account of the old man’s doings immediately afterwards; and of his own proceedings which had led him to believe for the time being that his suspicions were groundless.

===Chinese===
科林伍德对帕拉怀特失了兴致。此人看来同他挂心的那桩事并不相干。他坐下来，开始向埃尔德里克细讲自己当年在安东尼·巴特尔去世时对普拉特起的疑心；讲杰比·奈勒告诉他的、从《巴福德史》里取走的那张纸；讲小店伙描述的老头儿随后紧接着的种种举动；也讲他自己后来如何循线查访，一度叫自己相信那些疑心原是无根之谈。

===Original===
“But now,” he went on, “a new idea occurs to me. Suppose that that paper, found by my grandfather in a book which had certainly belonged to the late John Mallathorpe, was something important relating to Mrs. Mallathorpe? Suppose that my grandfather brought it across here to you? Suppose that finding you out, he showed it to Pratt? As my grandfather died suddenly, with nobody but Pratt there, what was there to prevent Pratt from appropriating that paper if he saw that it would give him a hold over Mrs. Mallathorpe? We know now that he has some document in his possession which does give him a hold﻿—may it not be that of which the boy Naylor told me?”

===Chinese===
“可是如今，”他接着说，“我脑子里又冒出一个新想法。倘若我祖父从一本书里翻出来的那张纸——那本书确凿是已故的约翰·马拉索普之物——是件同马拉索普太太大有干系的东西呢？倘若我祖父把它带到这边来，是来寻您的呢？倘若他寻访您时，却把它亮给了普拉特看呢？我祖父死得突然，当场除普拉特外再无旁人，普拉特若看出那张纸能让他攥住马拉索普太太的把柄，又有什么能拦住他据为己有？如今我们既已知道，他手里确有一份给他把柄的文书——那会不会正是小奈勒对我讲的那一张？”

===Original===
“Might be,” agreed Eldrick. “But﻿—my opinion is, taking things all together, that the paper which Antony Bartle found was the one you yourself discovered later﻿—the list of books. No﻿—I’ll tell you what I think. I believe that the document which Pratt told Miss Mallathorpe he holds, and to which her mother referred in the letter asking Pratt to meet her, is probably﻿—most probably!﻿—one which he discovered in searching out his relationship to Mrs. Mallathorpe. He’s a cute chap﻿—and he may have found some document which﻿—well, I’ll tell you what it might be﻿—something which would upset the rights of Harper Mallathorpe to his uncle’s estates. No other relatives came forward, or were heard of, or were discoverable when John Mallathorpe was killed in that chimney accident; but there may be some﻿—there may be one in particular. That’s my notion!﻿—and I intend, in the first place, to make a personal search of the parish registers from which Pratt got his information. He may have discovered something there which he’s keeping to himself.”

===Chinese===
“有这个可能，”埃尔德里克附和道。“不过——把种种情形归总掂量，我的看法是：安东尼·巴特尔找到的那张纸，正是你后来自己翻出来的那张——就是那份书目。不——我告诉你我是怎么想的。普拉特对马拉索普小姐自称握有的那份文书，也就是她母亲那封约普拉特会面的信里提到的那一份，多半——十有八九！——是他在查考自己同马拉索普太太的亲戚关系时发现的。他是个精明家伙——他可能翻出了某份文书，能够——唔，我告诉你那可能是什么——能掀翻哈珀·马拉索普继承他伯父产业的权柄。约翰·马拉索普在那场烟囱事故中丧生的时候，没有别的亲戚出面，也无人听说、无处寻访得到；可未必就没有——尤其可能有一位。这就是我的想法！——所以我打算，头一件事，亲自去查一遍普拉特获取消息所据的那些教区登记册。他可能在里面发现了什么，此刻还捂在自己一个人怀里。”

===Original===
“You think that is the course to adopt?” asked Collingwood, after a moment’s reflection.

===Chinese===
“您觉得就该走这一步？”科林伍德沉吟片刻，问道。

===Original===
“At present﻿—yes,” replied Eldrick. “And while I’m making it﻿—I’ll do it myself﻿—we’ll just go on outwardly﻿—as if nothing had happened. If I meet Pratt﻿—as I shall﻿—I shall not let him see that I know anything. Do you go on in just the usual way. Go out to Normandale Grange now and then﻿—and tell Miss Mallathorpe to think no more of her interview with Pratt until we’ve something to talk to her about. You talk to her about﻿—something else.”

===Chinese===
“眼下——是的，”埃尔德里克答道。“在我查访期间——我亲自去查——咱们表面上只管照常行事——只当什么事都没发生。我若碰见普拉特——这免不了——也绝不让他看出我知道了什么。你呢，只管一切照旧。隔三差五去诺曼代尔庄园走走——告诉马拉索普小姐，在咱们有话可跟她谈之前，先把她同普拉特那番对答丢开不想。你同她谈谈——别的什么。”

===Original===
When Collingwood had left him Eldrick laid a telegram form on his plotting pad, and after a brief interval of thought wrote out a message addressed to the people whose advertisement had attracted Pascoe’s attention.

===Chinese===
科林伍德走后，埃尔德里克把一张电报纸摊在吸墨垫上，凝神想了片刻，便拟好一封电文，收报人正是那则引得帕斯科留意的广告的登主。

===Original===
Halstead & Byner, 56B, St. Martin’s Chambers, London, W.C.

I can give you definite information concerning James Parrawhite if you will send representative to see me personally.

Charles Eldrick, Eldrick & Pascoe,

					Solicitors, Barford.

===Chinese===
哈尔斯特德—拜纳事务所，圣马丁事务室56B号，伦敦中西区

若贵方派代表前来与本人面见，我可提供有关詹姆斯·帕拉怀特的确切消息。

查尔斯·埃尔德里克，埃尔德里克—帕斯科

					律师事务所，巴福德。

===Original===
After Eldrick had sent off a clerk with this message to the nearest telegraph office, he sat thinking for some time. And at the close of his meditations, and after some turning over of a diary which lay on his desk, he picked up pen and paper, and drafted an advertisement of his own.

===Chinese===
埃尔德里克打发一名书记员拿这封电报去了最近的电报局，随后坐着思索了半晌。沉思到末了，又把摊在案头的一本日记翻检了一阵，他这才提起纸笔，草拟了一则自己的广告。

===Original===
**Ten Pounds Reward** will be paid to any person who can give reliable and useful information as to James Parrawhite, who until November last was a clerk in the employ of Messrs. Eldrick & Pascoe, Solicitors, Barford, and who is believed to have left the town on the evening of November 23.﻿—Apply to Mr. **Charles Eldrick**, of the above firm.

===Chinese===
**赏格十镑**：凡能就詹姆斯·帕拉怀特的下落提供可靠而有用的消息者，即付此数。该人迄至去年11月一直受雇于巴福德埃尔德里克—帕斯科律师事务所任书记员，据信已于11月23日傍晚离镇。请洽该事务所**查尔斯·埃尔德里克**先生。

===Original===
“Worth risking ten pounds on﻿—anyway,” muttered Eldrick. “Whether these London people will cover it or not. Here!” he went on, turning to a clerk who had just entered the room. “Make three copies of this advertisement, and take one to each of the three newspaper offices, and tell ’em to put it in their personal column tonight.”

===Chinese===
“这十镑花得值当冒险——横竖如此，”埃尔德里克咕哝道。“伦敦那班人认不认这笔账，另当别论。喂！”他接着说，转向刚进屋来的一个书记员。“把这则广告誊三份，三家报馆各送一份，叫他们登上今晚的私人启事栏。”

===Original===
He sat musing for some time after he was left alone again, and when he at last rose, it was with a shake of the head.

===Chinese===
等他又一次独坐屋中，还闷闷地出了半天神；末了起身时，不由得摇了摇头。

===Original===
“I wonder if Pratt told me the truth that morning?” he said to himself. “Anyway, he’s now being proved to be even deeper than I’d ever considered him. Well﻿—other folk than Pratt are possessed of pretty good wits.”

===Chinese===
“不知普拉特那天早上对我说的是不是实话？”他自言自语道。“不管怎样，如今看来，他比我对他的估量还要深得多。好在——这世上脑袋好使的，也不止普拉特一个。”

===Original===
Before he left the office that evening Eldrick was handed a telegram from Messrs. Halstead & Byner, of St. Martin’s Chambers, informing him that their Mr. Byner would travel to Barford by the first express next morning, and would call upon him at eleven o’clock.

===Chinese===
当晚离所之前，埃尔德里克接到圣马丁事务室哈尔斯特德—拜纳事务所发来的一封电报，告知他该所的拜纳先生将乘明早头班快车前来巴福德，十一点钟到所拜访。

===Original===
“Then they have some important news for Parrawhite,” mused Eldrick, as he put the message in his pocket and went off to his club. “Inquiry agents don’t set off on long journeys at a moment’s notice for a matter of a trifling agency. But﻿—where is Parrawhite?”

===Chinese===
“这么说，他们手里握着关于帕拉怀特的重要消息，”埃尔德里克把电报揣进口袋，往俱乐部去，一路寻思。“征信代理人断不至于为一桩芝麻大的委托，说走就走地赶长途。可是——帕拉怀特人在哪儿呢？”

===Original===
He awaited the arrival of Mr. Byner next morning with considerable curiosity. And soon after eleven there was shown in to him, a smart, well-dressed, alert-looking young man, who, having introduced himself as Mr. Gerald Byner, immediately plunged into business.

===Chinese===
第二天早上，他怀着相当的好奇等候拜纳先生到来。十一点刚过，有人引进来一位精明伶俐、衣着讲究、满面机警的年轻人；他自报家门，说是杰拉尔德·拜纳（Gerald Byner）先生，随即一头扎进了正题。

===Original===
“You can tell me something of James Parrawhite, Mr. Eldrick?” he began. “We shall be glad﻿—we’ve been endeavouring to trace him for some months. It’s odd that you didn’t see our advertisement before.”

===Chinese===
“关于詹姆斯·帕拉怀特，您能告诉我一些情况吧，埃尔德里克先生？”他开门见山。“那再好不过——我们寻他已经有好几个月了。真怪，您先前竟没瞧见我们的广告。”

===Original===
“I don’t look at that sort of advertisement,” replied Eldrick. “I believe it was by mere accident that my partner saw yours yesterday afternoon. But now, a question or two first. What are you﻿—inquiry agents?”

===Chinese===
“那一类广告我素来不看，”埃尔德里克答道。“我合伙人昨天下午瞧见你们那则，我想纯属碰巧。不过，先请教一两个问题。你们是干什么的——征信代理人？”

===Original===
“Just so, sir﻿—inquiry agents﻿—with a touch of private detective business,” answered Mr. Gerald Byner with a smile. “We undertake to find people, to watch people, to recover lost property, and so on. In this case we’re acting for Messrs. Vickers, Marshall & Hebbleton, Solicitors, of Cannon Street. They want James Parrawhite badly.”

===Chinese===
“正是，先生——征信代理——兼营一点私家侦探的营生，”杰拉尔德·拜纳先生含笑答道。“找人、盯人、寻回失物等等，我们都承接。这桩案子里，我们代理的是坎农街的维克斯—马歇尔—赫布尔顿律师事务所（Vickers, Marshall & Hebbleton）。他们急着要找到詹姆斯·帕拉怀特。”

===Original===
“Why?” asked Eldrick.

===Chinese===
“为什么要找他？”埃尔德里克问。

===Original===
“Because,” replied Byner with a dry laugh, “there’s about twenty thousand pounds waiting for him, in their hands.”

===Chinese===
“因为，”拜纳干笑一声答道，“他们手里正替他存着两万镑上下的一笔钱呢。”

===Original===
Eldrick whistled with astonishment.

===Chinese===
埃尔德里克惊得吹了声口哨。

===Original===
“Whew!” he said. “Twenty thousand﻿—for Parrawhite! My good sir﻿—if that’s so, and if, as you say, you’ve been advertising﻿—”

===Chinese===
“嚯！”他说。“两万镑——给帕拉怀特！我的好先生——倘若果真如此，又照您说的，你们一直在登广告——”

===Original===
“Advertising in several papers,” interrupted Byner. “Dailies, weeklies, provincials. Never had one reply, till your wire.”

===Chinese===
“好几家报纸都登了，”拜纳插话道。“日报、周报、外省报。直到您这封电报，一个回音都没得过。”

===Original===
“Then﻿—Parrawhite must be dead!” said Eldrick. “Or﻿—in gaol, under another name. Twenty thousand pounds﻿—waiting for Parrawhite! If Parrawhite was alive, man, or at liberty, he wouldn’t let twenty thousand pence wait five minutes! I know him!”

===Chinese===
“那么——帕拉怀特必定死了！”埃尔德里克说。“要么——就是顶着别的名字蹲了监狱。两万镑——候着帕拉怀特！老兄，帕拉怀特若是活着，或是自由之身，连两万便士他也等不得五分钟！我太了解这个人了！”

===Original===
“What can you tell me, Mr. Eldrick?” asked the inquiry agent.

===Chinese===
“您能告诉我些什么呢，埃尔德里克先生？”征信代理人问。

===Original===
Eldrick told all he knew﻿—concealing nothing. And Byner listened silently and eagerly.

===Chinese===
埃尔德里克把知道的一股脑儿全讲了——毫不隐瞒。拜纳一声不响地听着，听得极专注。

===Original===
“There’s something strikes me at once,” he said. “You say that with him disappeared three or four ten-pound notes of yours. Have you the numbers of those notes?”

===Chinese===
“有一桩事我立时就上了心，”他说。“您说随着他一同失踪的，还有您的三四张十镑纸币。那些纸币的编号，您手头有吗？”

===Original===
“I can’t say,” replied Eldrick, doubtfully. “I haven’t, certainly. But﻿—they were paid in to our head-clerk, Pratt, and I think he used to enter such things in a sort of day-ledger. I’ll get it.”

===Chinese===
“说不准，”埃尔德里克迟疑着答道。“我手头肯定没有。不过——那些票子当初是经我们主任书记员普拉特之手收进来的，我想这类票号他向来登在一本流水日记账上。我去取来。”

===Original===
He went into the clerks’ office and presently returned with an oblong, marble-backed book which he began to turn over.

===Chinese===
他走进书记员办公室，不多时捧回一本长方形、大理石纹封面的簿子，动手翻检起来。

===Original===
“This may be what you ask about,” he said at last. “Here, under date November 23, are some letters and figures which obviously refer to banknotes. You can copy them if you like.”

===Chinese===
“这多半就是您问的东西，”他末了说道。“喏，11月23日那天的日期底下，有几组字母同数字，显见得指的是钞票。您要抄便抄去。”

===Original===
“Another question, Mr. Eldrick,” remarked Byner as he made a note of the entries. “You say some cheque forms were abstracted from a book of yours at the same time. Have you ever heard of any of these cheque forms being made use of?”

===Chinese===
“再问一句，埃尔德里克先生，”拜纳一面把那几笔账目记下来，一面说。“您说与此同时，还从您的一本支票簿里抽走了几张空白支票。这些空白支票后来被人动用，您可曾听到过风声？”

===Original===
“Never!” replied Eldrick.

===Chinese===
“从来没有！”埃尔德里克答道。

===Original===
“No forgery of your name or anything?” suggested the caller.

===Chinese===
“没人伪造您的签名，或是诸如此类的事？”来客提示道。

===Original===
“No,” said Eldrick. “There’s been nothing of that sort.”

===Chinese===
“没有，”埃尔德里克说。“那一类事一件也没出过。”

===Original===
“I can soon ascertain if these banknotes have reached the Bank of England,” said Byner. “That’s a simple matter. Now suppose they haven’t!”

===Chinese===
“这几张纸币到没到过英格兰银行，我不难查明，”拜纳说。“那是桩简单事。可要是它们压根儿没到过呢！”

===Original===
“Well?” asked Eldrick.

===Chinese===
“哦？”埃尔德里克问。

===Original===
“You know, of course,” continued Byner, “that it doesn’t take long for a Bank of England note, once issued, to get back to the Bank? You know, too, that it’s never issued again. Now if those notes haven’t been presented at the Bank﻿—where are they? And if no use has been made of your stolen cheques﻿—where are they?”

===Chinese===
“您当然知道，”拜纳接着说，“英格兰银行的纸币一经发出，要不了多久就会流回银行？您也知道，回行的钞票绝不再发出去。如今那几张纸币若不曾有人拿到银行兑过——它们在哪儿？您被偷的支票若一直没被人动用——它们又在哪儿？”

===Original===
“Good!” agreed Eldrick. “I see that you ought to do well in your special line of business. Now﻿—are you going to pursue inquiries for Parrawhite here in Barford, after what I’ve told you?”

===Chinese===
“好！”埃尔德里克深以为然。“看得出，您干这一行注定错不了。那么——听了我这一番话之后，您还打算在巴福德这儿继续查访帕拉怀特吗？”

===Original===
“Certainly!” said Byner. “I came down prepared to stop awhile. It’s highly important that this man should be found﻿—highly important,” he added smiling, “to other people than Parrawhite himself.”

===Chinese===
“当然！”拜纳说。“我这次下来，就备好要多待些日子。找到此人是头等要紧的事——头等要紧，”他含笑补了一句，“对帕拉怀特以外的人而言，尤其如此。”

===Original===
“In what way?” asked Eldrick.

===Chinese===
“这话怎么讲？”埃尔德里克问。

===Original===
“Why,” replied Byner, “if he’s dead﻿—as he may be﻿—this money goes to somebody else﻿—a relative. The relative would be very glad to hear he is dead! But﻿—definite news will be welcome, in any case. Oh, yes, now that I’ve got down here, I shall do my best to trace him. You have the address of the woman he lodged with, you say. I shall go there first, of course. Then I must try to find out what he did with himself in his spare time. But, from all you tell me, it’s my impression he’s dead﻿—unless, as you say, he’s got into prison again﻿—possibly under another name. It seems impossible that he should not have seen our advertisements.”

===Chinese===
“呃，”拜纳答道，“他若是死了——这大有可能——这笔钱就落到别人头上——落给一位亲戚。那位亲戚若是听说他死了，怕不高兴坏了！不过——无论生死，确切的消息总是受欢迎的。哦，是了，既然人都到了这儿，我自当竭力追查他的踪迹。您说您有他寄宿的那位房东太太的地址。我自然头一处就上那儿去。然后我得设法打听打听，他闲空的时辰都拿自己怎么打发。不过，照您说的这一切看来，我的感觉是：他已经死了——除非像您说的，他又进了班房——兴许顶着另一个名字。要说我们的广告他竟一张都没瞧见，那似乎不可能。”

===Original===
“You never advertised in any Yorkshire newspapers?” asked Eldrick.

===Chinese===
“约克郡的报纸，你们一张也没登过？”埃尔德里克问。

===Original===
“No,” said Byner. “Because we’d no knowledge of his having come so far North. We advertised in the Midland papers. But then, all the London papers, daily and weekly, that we used come down to Yorkshire.”

===Chinese===
“没有，”拜纳说。“因为我们不知道他跑到了这么北边来。我们登的是中部各郡的报纸。不过话又说回来，我们用的那些伦敦报纸，日报周报，都会流通到约克郡来。”

===Original===
“Parrawhite,” said Eldrick reflectively, “was a big newspaper reader. He used to go to the Free Library reading-room a great deal. I begin to think he must certainly be dead﻿—or locked up. However, in supplement of your endeavours, I did a little work of my own last night. There you are!” he went on, picking up the local papers and handing them over. “I put that in﻿—we’ll see if any response comes. But now a word, Mr. Byner, since you’ve come to me. You have heard me mention my late clerk﻿—Pratt?”

===Chinese===
“帕拉怀特，”埃尔德里克若有所思地说，“是个顶爱读报的人。他往常老往免费图书馆的阅览室跑。我渐渐觉得，他必定是死了——要么就是关起来了。不过，在您的查访之外，昨晚我自己也小小地干了一手。喏，您瞧！”他接着说，拿起那几份本地报纸递过去。“我登了这么一条——看有没有回音再说。不过，拜纳先生，既然您到了我这里，我还有一句话。您听见我提起过我从前的主任书记员——普拉特了吧？”

===Original===
“Yes,” answered Byner.

===Chinese===
“听见了，”拜纳答道。

===Original===
“Pratt has left us, and is in business as a sort of estate agent in the next street,” continued Eldrick. “Now I have particular reasons﻿—most particular reasons!﻿—why Pratt should remain in absolute ignorance of your presence in the town. If you should happen to come across him﻿—as you may, for though there are a quarter of a million of us here, it’s a small place, compared with London﻿—don’t let him know your business.”

===Chinese===
“普拉特已经离开了我们所里，在隔壁一条街上做起了地产经理人那一类的营生，”埃尔德里克接着说。“如今我有特别的缘由——极其特别的缘由！——绝不能让普拉特知道您来到了本镇。您若是碰巧撞见他——这很有可能，咱们这儿虽有二十五万人口，比起伦敦终究是个小地方——也千万别让他知道您的来路。”

===Original===
“I’m not very likely to do that, Mr. Eldrick,” remarked Byner quietly.

===Chinese===
“这种事我原本就很少可能去干，埃尔德里克先生，”拜纳平静地说。

===Original===
“Aye, but you don’t take my meaning,” said Eldrick eagerly. “I mean this﻿—it’s just possible that Pratt may see that advertisement of yours, and that he may write to your firm. In that case, as he’s here, and you’re here, your partner would send his letter to you. Don’t deal with it﻿—here. Don’t﻿—if you should come across Pratt, even let him know your name!”

===Chinese===
“哎，可您没领会我的意思，”埃尔德里克急切地说。“我是这个意思——普拉特说不定会看见你们那则广告，说不定会写信给贵所。果真那样，他在此地，您也在此地，您的合伙人准会把他的信转给您。别在此地处理这封信。还有——您若当真撞见普拉特，连自己的姓名也别让他知道！”

===Original===
“When I’ve a job of this sort,” replied Byner, “I don’t let anybody know my name﻿—except people like you. When I register at one of your hotels presently, I shall be Mr. Black of London. But﻿—if this Pratt wanted to give any information about Parrawhite, he’d give it to you, surely, now that you’ve advertised.”

===Chinese===
“接了这一类差使，”拜纳答道，“我的姓名谁也不让知道——除了您这样的当事人。待会儿我到贵处哪家旅馆登记时，我就是伦敦来的布莱克先生。不过——这位普拉特若想提供帕拉怀特的消息，如今您既登了广告，他自然会报到您这儿来吧。”

===Original===
“No, he wouldn’t!” asserted Eldrick. “Why? Because he’s told me all he knows﻿—or says he knows﻿—already!”

===Chinese===
“不，他不会！”埃尔德里克断然道。“为什么？因为凡是他知道的——或者他自称知道的——他早已对我交底了！”

===Original===
The inquiry agent looked keenly at the solicitor for a moment during which they both kept silence. Then Byner smiled.

===Chinese===
征信代理人锐利地打量了律师片刻，这一会儿两人都没作声。随后拜纳笑了。

===Original===
“You said﻿—‘or says he knows,’ ” he remarked. “Do you think he didn’t tell the truth about Parrawhite?”

===Chinese===
“您方才说了——‘或者他自称知道的’，”他指出。“您认为他在帕拉怀特的事上没说实话？”

===Original===
“I should say﻿—now﻿—it’s quite likely he didn’t,” answered Eldrick. “The truth is, I’m making some inquiry myself about Pratt﻿—and I don’t want this to interfere with it. You keep me informed of what you find out, and I’ll help you all I can while you’re here. It may be﻿—”

===Chinese===
“要我说——如今看来——他多半是没说实话，”埃尔德里克答道。“实情是，我自己也正在对普拉特作些查访——我不愿这件事搅了它。您查出什么随时知会我，您在此期间我必全力相助。说不定——”

===Original===
A clerk came into the room and looked at his master.

===Chinese===
一名书记员进屋来，望着主人。

===Original===
“Mr. George Pickard, of the Green Man at Whitcliffe, sir,” he said.

===Chinese===
“惠特克利夫（Whitcliffe）‘绿人’酒馆的乔治·皮卡德（George Pickard）先生到，先生，”他说。

===Original===
“Well?” asked Eldrick.

===Chinese===
“什么事？”埃尔德里克问。

===Original===
“Wants to see you about that advertisement in the paper this morning, sir,” continued the clerk.

===Chinese===
“要见您，为的是今早报上那则广告，先生，”书记员接着说。

===Original===
Eldrick looked at Byner and smiled significantly. Then he turned towards the door.

===Chinese===
埃尔德里克望了拜纳一眼，意味深长地笑了。随后他朝门口转过身去。

===Original===
“Bring Mr. Pickard in,” he said.

===Chinese===
“请皮卡德先生进来，”他说。

## XVIII. The Confiding Landlord / 十八 吐露隐情的房东

===Original===
The clerk presently ushered in a short, thickset, round-faced man, apparently of thirty to thirty-five years of age, whose chief personal characteristics lay in a pair of the smallest eyes ever set in a human countenance and a mere apology for a nose. But both nose and eyes combined somehow to communicate an idea of profound inquiry as the round face in which they were placed turned from the solicitor to the man from London, and a podgy forefinger was lifted to a red forehead.

===Chinese===
书记员不多时引进来一个五短身材、敦实粗壮的圆脸汉子，年纪约莫在三十到三十五岁之间；他这副仪表，最要紧的特点全落在一双生在人脸上再小不过的小眼睛，外加一只聊胜于无的鼻子上。可是鼻子同眼睛两下里一凑合，竟不知怎么传递出一种深究细诘的意味——那张安放它们的圆脸从律师转向这位伦敦来客，一只圆胖的食指也举到了红彤彤的额头上。

===Original===
“Servant, gentlemen,” said the visitor. “Fine morning for the time of year!”

===Chinese===
“给二位先生请安了，”来客说道。“一年里这个时节，难得这么个好早晨！”

===Original===
“Take a chair, Mr. Pickard,” replied Eldrick. “Let me see﻿—from the Green Man, at Whitcliffe, I believe?”

===Chinese===
“请坐，皮卡德先生，”埃尔德里克答道。“让我想想——您是从惠特克利夫‘绿人’酒馆来的，我没猜错吧？”

===Original===
“Landlord, sir﻿—had that house a many years,” answered Pickard, as he took a seat near the wall. “Seven year come next Michaelmas, any road.”

===Chinese===
“是小店主，先生——那所房子俺掌了许多年了，”皮卡德一面在墙边落座，一面答道。“横竖到来年米迦勒节，就满七个年头了。”

===Original===
“Just so﻿—and you want to see me about the advertisement in this morning’s paper?” continued Eldrick. “What about it﻿—now?”

===Chinese===
“正是——那么，您是要为今早报上那则广告的事见我了？”埃尔德里克接着说。“到底是什么事——说吧？”

===Original===
The landlord looked at Eldrick and then at Eldrick’s companion. The solicitor understood that look: it meant that what his caller had to say was of a private nature.

===Chinese===
店主看看埃尔德里克，又看看埃尔德里克的同伴。律师看懂了那眼色：它的意思是，来客要讲的话属于私事。

===Original===
“It’s all right, Mr. Pickard,” he remarked reassuringly. “This gentleman is here on just the same business﻿—whatever you say will be treated as confidential﻿—it’ll go no further. You’ve something to tell about my late clerk, James Parrawhite.”

===Chinese===
“不妨事的，皮卡德先生，”他宽解道。“这位先生正是为同一桩事来的——您说的一切都将作为机密——绝不会再传出去半句。您是有关于我从前那个书记员詹姆斯·帕拉怀特的事，要讲一讲吧。”

===Original===
Pickard, who had been nervously fingering a white billycock hat, now put it down on the floor and thrust his hands into the pockets of his trousers as if to keep them safe while he talked.

===Chinese===
皮卡德先前一直紧张地捻弄着一顶白色圆顶毡帽，这时把它搁到地板上，双手插进裤兜，仿佛要叫这双手在他讲话的当口老实待着。

===Original===
“It’s like this here,” he answered. “When I saw that there advertisement in the paper this mornin’, says I to my missus, ‘I’ll away,’ I says, ‘an’ see Lawyer Eldrick about that there, this very day!’ ’Cause you see, Mr. Eldrick, there is summat as I can tell about yon man ’at you mention﻿—James Parrawhite. I’ve said nowt about it to nobody, up to now, ’cause it were private business atween him and me, as it were, but I lost money over it, and of course, ten pound is ten pound, gentlemen.”

===Chinese===
“事情是这么一档子事，”他答道。“今儿早上俺在报上瞧见那则广告，俺就跟俺家里的说：‘俺得走一趟，’俺说，‘当天就去见埃尔德里克律师，问问那桩事！’您瞧，埃尔德里克先生，您提起的那位——詹姆斯·帕拉怀特——俺这儿倒真有些事可以讲讲。到今儿为止，俺跟谁也没露过一个字，因为那原是俺同他两个人之间的私事，这么说吧；可俺在这上头蚀了钱，再说，十镑终归是十镑呀，二位先生。”

===Original===
“Quite so,” agreed Eldrick. “And you shall have your ten pounds if you can tell anything useful.”

===Chinese===
“正是这么回事，”埃尔德里克附和道。“只要您能讲出些有用的东西，那十镑您就挣着了。”

===Original===
“I don’t know owt about it’s being useful, sir, nor what use is to be made on it,” said Pickard, “but I can tell you a bit o’ truth, and you can do what you like wi’ what I tell. But,” he went on, lowering his voice and glancing at the door by which he had just entered, “there’s another name ’at’ll have to be browt in﻿—private, like. Name, as it so happens, o’ one o’ your clerks﻿—t’ head clerk, I’m given to understand﻿—Mr. Pratt.”

===Chinese===
“这话有用没用，俺可不晓得，先生，也不晓得您拿它派什么用场，”皮卡德说，“可俺能给您讲一段实话，俺讲出来的，随您拿去怎么使唤都成。不过，”他接着往下说，嗓门压低了，还瞟了一眼自己刚进来的那扇门，“还得把另一个名字扯进来——私底下讲讲。那名字，不早不晚，偏偏是贵所一位书记员的大名——听人家说，是主任书记员——普拉特先生。”

===Original===
Eldrick showed no sign of surprise. But he continued to look significantly at Byner as he turned to the landlord.

===Chinese===
埃尔德里克没有露出一丝诧异。只是他转向店主时，照旧意味深长地望着拜纳。

===Original===
“Mr. Pratt has left me,” he said. “Left me three weeks ago. So you needn’t be afraid, Mr. Pickard﻿—say anything you like.”

===Chinese===
“普拉特先生已经离开我了，”他说。“三个礼拜前走的。所以您不必担心，皮卡德先生——想说什么只管说。”

===Original===
“Oh, I didn’t know,” remarked Pickard. “It’s not oft that I come down in t’ town, and we don’t hear much Barford news up our way. Well, it’s this here, Mr. Eldrick﻿—you know where my place is, of course?”

===Chinese===
“哦，这个俺可不知道，”皮卡德说。“俺难得进一回城，俺们那头也听不着多少巴福德的消息。唔，事情是这样，埃尔德里克先生——俺那家店在什么地方，您自然是知道的？”

===Original===
Eldrick nodded, and turned to Byner.

===Chinese===
埃尔德里克点了点头，转向拜纳。

===Original===
“I’d better explain to you,” he said. “Whitcliffe is an outlying part of the town, well up the hills﻿—a sort of wayside hamlet with a lot of our famous stone quarries in its vicinity. The Green Man, of which our friend here is the landlord, is an old-fashioned tavern by the roadside﻿—where people are rather fond of dropping in on a Sunday, I fancy, eh, Mr. Pickard?”

===Chinese===
“我最好跟您说明一下，”他说。“惠特克利夫是本镇边上的一处地方，高高地在山冈上——算是个大路旁的小村坊，四近尽是咱们这儿有名的那些采石场。咱们这位朋友做店东的‘绿人’，是路旁一家老派酒馆——礼拜天人们满爱顺脚进去坐坐的，我想，呃，皮卡德先生？”

===Original===
“You’re right, sir,” replied the landlord. “It makes a nice walk out on a Sunday. And it were on a Sunday, too, ’at I got to know this here James Parrawhite as you want to know summat about. He began coming to my place of a Sunday evenin’, d’ye see, gentlemen?﻿—he’d walk across t’ valley up there to Whitcliffe and stop an hour or two, enjoyin’ hisself. Well, now, as you’re no doubt well aweer, Mr. Eldrick, he were a reight hand at talkin’, were yon Parrawhite﻿—he’d t’ gift o’ t’ gab reight enough, and talked well an’ all. And of course him an’ me, we hed bits o’ conversation at times, ’cause he come to t’ house reg’lar and sometimes o’ weeknights an’ all. An’ he tell’d me ’at he’d had a deal o’ experience i’ racin’ matters﻿—whether it were true or not, I couldn’t say, but﻿—”

===Chinese===
“您说得对，先生，”店主答道。“礼拜天出来上那儿走走，是怪惬意的。俺认识这位詹姆斯·帕拉怀特——就是您几位要打听的那一位——也正是在一个礼拜天。他起先是每逢礼拜天傍晚上俺店里来，您明白吗，二位先生？——他从山谷底下那头一路爬上来，走到惠特克利夫，待上一两个钟头，自得其乐。唔，埃尔德里克先生，这层您想必早就清楚：那位帕拉怀特真是个说话的行家——好口才他是真有，还说得头头是道。一来二去，俺同他自然不时搭上几句，因为他上店里来得上勤，有时平日工作日的晚上也来。他还告诉俺，说他在赛马那些事体上头有过老些经验——是真是假，俺说不上来，可是——”

===Original===
“True enough!” said Eldrick. “He had.”

===Chinese===
“千真万确！”埃尔德里克说。“他确实有过。”

===Original===
“Well, so he said,” continued Pickard, “and he was allus tellin’ me ’at he could make a pile o’ brass on t’ turf if he only had capital. An’ i’ t’ end, he persuaded me to start what he called investin’ money with him i’ that way﻿—i’ plain language, it meant givin’ him brass to put on horses ’at he said was goin’ to win, d’ye understand?”

===Chinese===
“唔，他是这么说的，”皮卡德接着讲，“他还总跟俺念叨，说他但凡有本钱，就能在马场上挣下老大一堆铜钿。到末了，他说动了俺，起头搞他所谓的把钱‘投’到他那一行里去——说白了，就是把铜钿交给他，押到他说要赢的马身上，您听明白没有？”

===Original===
“Perfectly,” replied Eldrick. “You gave him various amounts which he was to stake for you.”

===Chinese===
“听得明明白白，”埃尔德里克答道。“您陆续给了他好些数目不等的钱，让他替您下注。”

===Original===
“Just so, sir! And at first,” said Pickard, with a shake of the head, “at first I’d no great reason to grumble. He cert’ny wor a good hand at spottin’ a winner. But as time went on, I’ t’ greatest difficulty in gettin’ a settlement wi’ him, d’ye see? He wor just as good a hand at makin’ excuses as he wor at pickin’ out winners﻿—better, I think! I nivver knew wheer I was wi’ him﻿—he’d pay up, and then he’d persuade me to go in for another do wi’ t’ brass I’d won, and happen we should lose that time, and then of course we had to hev another investment to get back what we’d dropped, and so it went on. But t’ end wor this here﻿—last November theer wor about fifty to sixty pound o’ mine i’ his hands, and I wanted it. I’d a spirit merchant’s bill to settle, and I wanted t’ brass badly for that. I knew Parrawhite had been paid, d’ye see, by t’ turf agent, ’at he betted wi’, and I plagued him to hand t’ brass over to me. He made one excuse and then another﻿—howsumivver, it come to that very day you’re talkin’ about i’ your advertisement, Mr. Eldrick﻿—the twenty-third o’ November﻿—”

===Chinese===
“正是这么档子事，先生！起先哪，”皮卡德摇着头说，“起先俺还真没什么大可抱怨的。挑中赢马，他着实是把手。可是日子一长，您瞧，俺要同他结一回账，得费九牛二虎之力。寻借口的功夫，同他挑赢头的本事一样到家——俺看还更胜一筹！同他搅在一处，俺从来闹不清自己是赚是赔——他结一回账，跟着又撺掇俺拿赢来的铜钿再干一注，兴许那一回又输了，那当然就只好再投一笔，好把赔出去的捞回来，就这么滚个没完没了。可到末了是这么个下场——去年11月，落在他手里的钱有五十到六十镑是俺的，俺要讨回来。俺有一张酒商的账单等着清付，那笔铜钿俺急等着使。俺知道帕拉怀特已经从他对赌的那个赛马经纪人手里收着了钱，您明白吗，俺就死缠着他，要他把铜钿交到俺手上。他推了这一桩又推那一桩——不管怎么说，日子偏偏就撞上您广告里说的那一天了，埃尔德里克先生——11月23日——”

===Original===
“Stop a minute, Mr. Pickard,” interrupted Eldrick. “Now, how do you know﻿—for a certainty﻿—that this day you’re going to talk about was the twenty-third of November?”

===Chinese===
“且慢，皮卡德先生，”埃尔德里克插话道。“您要讲的这一天，您是怎么知道——确确切切地知道——就是11月23日的？”

===Original===
The landlord, who had removed his hands from his pockets, and was now twiddling a pair of fat thumbs as he talked, chuckled slyly.

===Chinese===
店主早已把双手从裤兜里抽出来，这会儿一面说话，一面捻搓着一对胖拇指，满脸狡黠地嘿嘿直笑。

===Original===
“For a very good reason,” he answered. “I had to pay that spirit bill I tell’d about just now on t’ twenty-fourth, and that I’m going to tell you happened t’ night afore t’ twenty-fourth, so of course it were t’ twenty-third. D’ye see?”

===Chinese===
“这里头有个再硬不过的因由，”他答道。“俺方才说的那张酒商账单，俺得在24号那天付清；而俺就要讲给您听的事，出在24号的前一夜，所以那自然就是23号。您瞧出来没有？”

===Original===
“I see,” asserted Eldrick. “That’ll do! And now﻿—what did happen?”

===Chinese===
“瞧出来了，”埃尔德里克断言道。“这就够了！那么——究竟出了什么事？”

===Original===
“This here,” replied Pickard. “On that night﻿—t’ twenty-third November﻿—Parrawhite came into t’ Green Man at about, happen, half-past eight. He come into t’ little private parlour to me, bold as brass﻿—as indeed, he allers wor. ‘Ye’re a nice un!’ I says. ‘I’ve written yer three letters durin’ t’ last week, and ye’ve nivver answered one o’ ’em!’ ‘I’ve come to answer i’ person,’ he says. ‘There’s nobbut one answer I want,’ says I. ‘Wheer’s my money?’ ‘Now then, be quiet a bit,’ he says. ‘You shall have your money before the evening’s over,’ he says. ‘Or, if not, as soon as t’ banks is open tomorrow mornin’,’ he says. ‘Wheer’s it coomin’ from?’ says I. ‘Now, never you mind,’ he says. ‘It’s safe!’ ‘I don’t believe a word you’re sayin’,’ says I. ‘Ye’re havin’ me for t’ mug!﻿—that’s about it.’ An’ I went on so at him, ’at i’ t’ end he tell’d me ’at he wor presently goin’ to meet Pratt, and ’at he could get t’ brass out o’ Pratt an’ as much more as iwer he liked to ax for. Well, I don’t believe that theer, and I said so. ‘What brass has Pratt?’ says I. ‘Pratt’s nowt but a clerk, wi’ happen three or four pound a week!’ ‘That’s all you know,’ he says. ‘Pratt’s become a gold mine, and I’m going to dig in it a bit. What’s it matter to you,’ he says, ‘so long as you get your brass?’ Well, of course, that wor true enough﻿—all ’at I wanted just then were to handle my brass. And I tell’d him so. ‘I’ll brek thy neck, Parrawhite,’ I says, ‘if thou doesn’t bring me that theer money eyther tonight or t’ first thing tomorrow﻿—so now!’ ‘Don’t talk rot!’ he says. ‘I’ve told you!’ And he had money wi’ him then﻿—‘nough to pay for drinks and cigars, any road, and we had a drink or two, and a smoke or two, and then he went out, sayin’ he wor goin’ to meet Pratt, and he’d be back at my place before closin’ time wi’ either t’ cash or what ’ud be as good. An’ I waited﻿—and waited after closin’ time, an’ all. But I’ve nivver seen Parrawhite from that day to this﻿—nor heerd tell on him neither!”

===Chinese===
“事情是这么一桩，”皮卡德答道。“那天夜里——11月23日——帕拉怀特来到‘绿人’，钟点嘛，就算八点半上下吧。他径直进小雅间里来寻俺，那份老脸皮、那副理直气壮的架势——说实在的，他向来就是这副做派。‘你可真是个好样的！’俺说。‘上礼拜俺给你写了三封信，你一封都没回！’‘我这不来当面回话了嘛，’他说。‘俺要的回话只有一句，’俺说。‘俺的钱在哪儿？’‘得得，你先消停一会儿，’他说。‘不等今晚天黑透，你的钱准到手，’他说。‘再不然，明天早上银行一开门，你也就拿到了，’他说。‘钱打哪儿来？’俺说。‘喏，这个你就别管了，’他说。‘稳稳当当！’‘你说的话俺一个字也不信，’俺说。‘你是拿俺当冤大头耍！——就是这么回事。’俺就这么劈头盖脸地数落他，数落到末了，他才告诉俺，说他这就去会普拉特，说他能从普拉特身上抠出铜钿来，他乐意开多少口，就能抠出多少。唔，这话俺才不信，俺也这么明说了。‘普拉特有什么铜钿？’俺说。‘普拉特不就是个书记员嘛，一礼拜顶天三四个镑！’‘那是因为你不知道，’他说。‘普拉特如今成了座金矿，俺正打算进去挖上一锹。这关你什么事，’他说，‘反正你自己的铜钿到手不就结了？’唔，这话倒不假——俺那阵子图的，本来就是把俺的铜钿攥到自己手里。俺也这么跟他说了。‘俺拧断你的脖子，帕拉怀特，’俺说，‘今儿夜里要不拿钱来，明儿一早也必须送到——话就撂在这儿！’‘别说浑话！’他说。‘我说过了！’再说那阵子他身上倒真带着钱——横竖付酒钱、雪茄钱是尽够的——俺们喝了一两杯，抽了一两支，随后他就出门了，说是去会普拉特，说打烊之前准回俺店里，要么带着现钱，要么带着一样顶得上现钱的东西。俺就等啊——打烊以后还接着等。可是打那天起到今儿，俺再没见过帕拉怀特——连他一点音信也没听人提起过！”

===Original===
Eldrick and Byner looked at each other for a moment. Then the solicitor spoke﻿—quietly and with a significance which the agent understood.

===Chinese===
埃尔德里克同拜纳对视了片刻。随后律师开了口——语气平静，话里有话，征信代理人心领神会。

===Original===
“Do you want to ask Mr. Pickard any questions?” he said.

===Chinese===
“您想不想问皮卡德先生几句？”他说。

===Original===
Byner nodded and turned to the landlord.

===Chinese===
拜纳点了点头，转向店主。

===Original===
“Did Parrawhite tell you where he was going to meet Pratt?” he asked.

===Chinese===
“帕拉怀特跟您说过他要去哪儿会普拉特吗？”他问。

===Original===
“He did,” replied Pickard. “Near Pratt’s lodgin’ place.”

===Chinese===
“说过，”皮卡德答道。“在普拉特寄宿的地方跟前。”

===Original===
“Did﻿—or does﻿—Pratt live near you, then?”

===Chinese===
“那么，普拉特那时候住得——或者说如今还住得——离您近吗？”

===Original===
“Closish by﻿—happen ten minutes’ walk. There’s few o’ houses﻿—a sort o’ terrace, like, on t’ edge o’ what they call Whitcliffe Moor. Pratt lodged﻿—lodges now for all I know to t’ contrary﻿—i’ one o’ them.”

===Chinese===
“不算远——走着十来分钟吧。那儿没几所房子——像是一溜排屋，就在他们唤作惠特克利夫荒原的边上。普拉特那阵子寄住——如今多半还住着，俺又没听说他搬了家——就住在其中一所里。”

===Original===
“Did Parrawhite give you any idea that he was going to the house in which Pratt lodged?”

===Chinese===
“帕拉怀特可曾向您透出过口风，说他要上普拉特寄住的那所房子去？”

===Original===
“No! He were not goin’ to t’ house. I know he worn’t. He tell’d me ’at he’d a good idea what time Pratt ’ud be home, ’cause he knew where he was that evening and he were goin’ to meet him just afore Pratt got to his place. I know where he’d meet him.”

===Chinese===
“没有！他不上房子里去。这个俺拿得准。他跟俺说，普拉特几点到家他心里大致有数，因为他晓得那天傍晚普拉特身在何处，他要赶在普拉特进家门之前截住他。他要在哪儿截他，俺也知道。”

===Original===
“Where?” asked Byner. “Tell me exactly. It’s important.”

===Chinese===
“在哪儿？”拜纳问。“原原本本说给我听。这事关紧要。”

===Original===
“Pratt ’ud come up fro’ t’ town i’ t’ tram,” answered Pickard. “He’d approach this here terrace I tell’d you about by a narrow lane that runs off t’ high road. He’d meet him there, would Parrawhite.”

===Chinese===
“普拉特从城里坐电车上来，”皮卡德答道。“要到俺方才讲的那溜排屋，他得从大路拐进一条窄巷子走。就在那儿，帕拉怀特会截住他。”

===Original===
“Did you ever ask any question of Pratt about Parrawhite?”

===Chinese===
“关于帕拉怀特，您可曾向普拉特打听过半句话？”

===Original===
“No﻿—never! I’d no wish that Pratt should know owt about my dealin’s with Parrawhite. When Parrawhite never come back﻿—why, I kep’ it all to myself, till now.”

===Chinese===
“没有——从来没有！俺压根儿不愿叫普拉特知道俺同帕拉怀特有那些银钱来往。后来帕拉怀特一去不回——唔，俺就一个人闷在肚里，直到今儿。”

===Original===
“What do you think happened to Parrawhite, Mr. Pickard?” asked Byner.

===Chinese===
“您琢磨帕拉怀特出了什么事，皮卡德先生？”拜纳问。

===Original===
“Gow, I know what I think!” replied Pickard disgustedly. “I think ’at if he did get any brass out o’ Pratt﻿—which is what I know nowt about, and hewn’t much belief in﻿—he went straight away fro’ t’ town﻿—vanished! I do know this﻿—he nivver went back to his lodgin’s that neet, ’cause I went theer mysen next day to inquire.”

===Chinese===
“嗐，俺心里怎么琢磨的，俺自己清楚！”皮卡德没好气地答道。“俺是这么琢磨的：他要是真从普拉特那儿抠着了铜钿——抠没抠着俺不知道，俺也不大信——他就径直出镇跑了——人没影儿了！有一桩俺是拿得准的——那天夜里他压根儿没回寄宿处，因为第二天俺亲自跑到那儿去打听过。”

===Original===
Eldrick pricked up his ears at that. He remembered that he had sent Pratt to make inquiry at Parrawhite’s lodgings on the morning whereon the money was missing.

===Chinese===
这话叫埃尔德里克竖起了耳朵。他记得，正是钱款失踪的那天早上，他打发普拉特去帕拉怀特的寄宿处打听过。

===Original===
“What time of the day﻿—on the twenty-fourth﻿—was that, Mr. Pickard?” he asked.

===Chinese===
“那是在——24号——一天里的什么时辰，皮卡德先生？”他问。

===Original===
“Evenin’, sir,” replied the landlord. “They’d nivver seen naught of him since he went out the day before. Oh, he did me, did Parrawhite! Of course, I lost mi brass﻿—fifty odd pounds!”

===Chinese===
“傍晚，先生，”店主答道。“打他头一天出门起，他们就再没见过他半点影儿。哦，帕拉怀特可把俺坑苦了，真真儿把俺坑苦了！不用说，俺的铜钿折了个精光——五十好几镑！”

===Original===
Byner gave Eldrick a glance.

===Chinese===
拜纳朝埃尔德里克递了个眼色。

===Original===
“I think Mr. Pickard has earned the ten pounds you offered,” he said.

===Chinese===
“我看，皮卡德先生挣得上您许的那十镑了，”他说。

===Original===
Eldrick took the hint and pulled out his chequebook.

===Chinese===
埃尔德里克听出了话音，掏出支票簿。

===Original===
“Of course, you’re to keep all this private﻿—strictly private, Mr. Pickard,” he said as he wrote. “Not a word to a soul!”

===Chinese===
“当然，这一切您得捂严实——严严实实，皮卡德先生，”他一面写一面说。“对哪个活人也休提半个字！”

===Original===
“Just as you order, sir,” agreed Pickard. “I’ll say nowt﻿—to nobody.”

===Chinese===
“全凭您吩咐，先生，”皮卡德应承道。“俺什么也不说——对谁也不说。”

===Original===
“And﻿—perhaps tomorrow﻿—perhaps this afternoon﻿—you’ll see me at the Green Man,” remarked Byner. “I shall just drop in, you know. You needn’t know me﻿—if there’s anybody about.”

===Chinese===
“还有——兴许明天——兴许今天下午——您就会在‘绿人’见到我，”拜纳说。“我只管顺脚进去坐坐，您知道。您只当不认得我——要是旁边有人的话。”

===Original===
“All right, sir﻿—I understand,” said Pickard.

===Chinese===
“成，先生——俺明白，”皮卡德说。

===Original===
“Quiet’s the word﻿—what? Very good﻿—much obliged to you, gentlemen.”

===Chinese===
“只一个字：缄口——是吧？好极——多谢二位先生了。”

===Original===
When the landlord had gone Eldrick motioned Byner to pick up his hat. “Come across the street with me,” he said. “I want us to have a consultation with a friend of mine, a barrister, Mr. Collingwood. For this matter is assuming a very queer aspect, and we can’t move too warily, nor consider all the features too thoroughly.”

===Chinese===
店主走后，埃尔德里克示意拜纳拿起帽子。“跟我一块儿过街去，”他说。“我要咱们去同我的一位朋友碰头商量——出庭律师科林伍德先生。这桩事眼下正显出一副万分古怪的面目，咱们行事，再怎么谨慎也不为过，各方面关节再怎么细究也不为过。”

===Original===
Collingwood listened with deep interest to Eldrick’s account of the morning’s events. And once again he was struck by the fact that all these various happenings in connection with Pratt, and now with Parrawhite, took place at the time of Antony Bartle’s death, and he said so.

===Chinese===
科林伍德怀着浓厚的兴致，听埃尔德里克讲了上午的种种经过。他再次被一个事实触动：这些同普拉特相关、如今又同帕拉怀特相关的种种变故，都出在安东尼·巴特尔去世的那段日子里。他也把这层照直说了出来。

===Original===
“True enough!” agreed Eldrick.

===Chinese===
“千真万确！”埃尔德里克附和道。

===Original===
“And once more,” pointed out Collingwood. “We’re hearing of a hold! Pratt claims to have a hold on Mrs. Mallathorpe﻿—now it turns out that Parrawhite boasted of a hold on Pratt. Suppose all these things have a common origin? Suppose the hold which Parrawhite had﻿—or has﻿—on Pratt is part and parcel of the hold which Pratt has on Mrs. Mallathorpe? In that case﻿—or cases﻿—what is the best thing to do?”

===Chinese===
“还有一层，”科林伍德指出。“咱们又听说了一个把柄！普拉特自称攥着马拉索普太太的把柄——如今又冒出来，帕拉怀特当年夸口攥着普拉特的把柄。倘若这些事全都出自同一个根苗呢？倘若帕拉怀特对普拉特的把柄——无论当年还是如今——正是普拉特对马拉索普太太那个把柄的一部分呢？果真这样——或者说这几桩事果真这样——那上策该怎么走？”

===Original===
“Will you gentlemen allow me to suggest something?” said Byner. “Very well﻿—find Parrawhite! Of all the people concerned in this, Parrawhite, from your account of him, anyway, Mr. Eldrick, is the likeliest person to extract the truth from.”

===Chinese===
“二位先生容我提个建议如何？”拜纳说。“说白了——找到帕拉怀特！这桩事里相关的众人之中，反正照您对他的那番描摹，埃尔德里克先生，帕拉怀特是最有指望从他嘴里掏出实情来的一个。”

===Original===
“There’s a great deal in that suggestion,” said Eldrick. “Do you know what I think?” he went on, turning to Collingwood, “Mr. Byner tells me he means to stay here until he has come across some satisfactory news of Parrawhite or solved the mystery of his disappearance. Well, now that we’ve found that there is some ground for believing that Parrawhite was in some fashion mixed up with Pratt about that time, why not place the whole thing in Mr. Byner’s hands﻿—let him in any case see what he can do about the Parrawhite-Pratt business of November twenty-third, eh?”

===Chinese===
“这个提议大有道理，”埃尔德里克说。“知道我是怎么想的吗？”他转向科林伍德，接着说，“拜纳先生告诉过我，他打算留在此地，直到撞见帕拉怀特什么像样的下落、或是解开他失踪之谜为止。唔，如今咱们既然发现，有理由相信帕拉怀特在那时候同普拉特有过某种牵连，何不把整桩事都交到拜纳先生手上——让他无论如何去查一查11月23日那桩帕拉怀特—普拉特的公案，看能查出个什么名堂，嗯？”

===Original===
“I take it,” answered Collingwood, looking at the inquiry agent, “that Mr. Byner having heard what he has, would do that quite apart from us?”

===Chinese===
“我料想，”科林伍德望着征信代理人答道，“拜纳先生既已听到了这些，撇开咱们，他自己也照样会去追查的吧？”

===Original===
“Yes,” said Byner. “Now that I’ve heard what Pickard had to say, I certainly shall follow that up.”

===Chinese===
“正是，”拜纳说。“如今我既听到了皮卡德这番话，这条线我断然要追下去。”

===Original===
“I am following out something of my own,” said Collingwood, turning to Eldrick. “I shall know more by this time tomorrow. Let us have a conference here﻿—at noon.”

===Chinese===
“我自己也正循着一条线在查，”科林伍德转向埃尔德里克说。“到明天这个时辰，我就知道得更多了。咱们就在这儿碰头商议——正午。”

===Original===
They separated on that understanding, and Byner went his own ways. His first proceeding was to visit, one after another, the Barford newspaper offices, and to order the insertion in large type, and immediately, of the Halstead-Byner advertisement for news of Parrawhite. His second was to seek the General Post Office, where he wrote out and dispatched a message to his partner in London. That message was in cipher﻿—translated into English, it read as follows:﻿—

===Chinese===
三人讲定就此分手，拜纳自去走他的路。他的头一件事，是挨家走访巴福德的各家报馆，吩咐以大号字体、即刻刊登哈尔斯特德—拜纳那则寻访帕拉怀特消息的广告。第二件事，是去寻邮政总局，在那里拟好一封发往伦敦合伙人的电文，随即拍发出去。那封电文用的是密码——译成英文，内容如下：——

===Original===
“If person named Pratt sends any communication to us re Parrawhite, on no account let him know I am in Barford, but forward whatever he sends to me at once, addressed to H. D. Black, Central Station Hotel.”

===Chinese===
“如有名唤普拉特之人就帕拉怀特一事向本所来函，切不可让他知道我人在巴福德，惟将其寄来的一切即刻转寄于我，收报人：H. D. 布莱克，中央车站旅馆。”

## XIX. The Eyewitness / 十九 目击者

===Original===
When Collingwood said that he was following out something of his own, he was thinking of an interesting discovery which he had made. It was one which might have no significance in relation to the present perplexities—on the other hand, out of it might come a good deal of illumination. Briefly, it was that on the evening before this consultation with Eldrick & Byner, he had found out that he was living in the house of a man who had actually witnessed the famous catastrophe at Mallathorpe’s Mill, whereby John Mallathorpe, his manager, and his cashier, together with some other bystanders, had lost their lives.

===Chinese===
科林伍德说自己正循着一条线在查的时候，心里想的其实是他新近有的一桩有趣发现。这发现同眼下的种种疑团也许毫不相干——可话说回来，从里头说不定能照出许多亮堂来。简单说，事情是这样：同埃尔德里克与拜纳碰头商议的前一天傍晚，他弄明白了一件事——他寄宿的这所房子，房东当年竟亲眼目睹过马拉索普工厂那场有名的大祸；约翰·马拉索普连同他的经理长、他的出纳主任，还有另外几个在场的人，都在那场祸事里送了命。

===Original===
On settling down in Barford, Collingwood had spent a couple of weeks in looking about him for comfortable rooms of a sort that appealed to his love of quiet and retirement. He had found them at last in an old house on the outskirts of the town—a fine old stone house, once a farmstead, set in a large garden, and tenanted by a middle-aged couple, who having far more room than they needed for themselves, had no objection to letting part of it to a business gentleman. Collingwood fell in love with this place as soon as he saw it. The rooms were large and full of delightful nooks and corners; the garden was rich in old trees; from it there were fine views of the valley beneath, and the heather-clad hills in the distance; within two miles of the town and easily approached by a convenient tram-route, it was yet quite out in the country.

===Chinese===
在巴福德安顿下来之后，科林伍德花了两个礼拜四处寻摸合意的寓所，要那种合乎他爱清静、喜幽居脾性的舒服房间。末了总算寻着了——镇郊一所老宅子，讲究的老石头房子，早先是个农庄院落，坐落在一方大花园里，住着一对中年夫妇；他们自家用不了那么多屋子，并不反对匀出一部分，租给一位有营生的先生。科林伍德一眼就看上了这地方。房间宽大，处处是讨人喜欢的犄角旮旯；园中古树葱茏；站在园里，脚下山谷的景致尽收眼底，远处是石楠丛生的山冈；离镇上不到两英里，一条便当的电车线直通，却又十足是乡野地界。

===Original===
He was just as much set up by his landlady—a comfortable, middle-aged woman, who fostered true Yorkshire notions about breakfast, and knew how to cook a good dinner at night. With her Collingwood had soon come to terms, and to his new abode had transferred a quantity of books and pictures from London. He soon became acquainted with the domestic ménage. There was the landlady herself, Mrs. Cobcroft, who, having no children of her own, had adopted a niece, now grown up, and a teacher in an adjacent elementary school: there was a strapping, rosy-cheeked servant-maid, whose dialect was too broad for the lodger to understand more than a few words of it; finally there was Mr. Cobcroft, a mild-mannered, quiet man who disappeared early in the morning, and was sometimes seen by Collingwood returning home in the evening.

===Chinese===
这地方叫他一见倾心，房东太太也同样叫他称心——一位舒舒服服的中年妇人，守着地道的约克郡人的早餐讲究，也知道晚上怎么烧出一顿好晚饭。科林伍德很快同她谈妥了，把一批书籍图画从伦敦运到了新居。不多久，这一家的家口他也熟识了。有房东太太本人科布克罗夫特太太——她自己没有儿女，收养了一个侄女，如今已长大成人，在邻近一所小学当教师；有一个高高大大、脸蛋红扑扑的女仆，一口土话重得厉害，房客能听懂的拢共没有几个字；末了还有科布克罗夫特先生，一个温文和气、不多言语的人，一清早就没了人影，科林伍德有时瞧见他傍晚转回家来。

===Original===
Lately, with the advancing spring, this unobtrusive individual was seen about the garden at the end of the day: Collingwood had so seen him on the evening before the talk with Eldrick and Byner, busied in setting seeds in the flowerbeds. And he had asked Mrs. Cobcroft, just then in his sitting-room, if her husband was fond of gardening.

===Chinese===
开春以来，天色向晚的时候，常能瞧见这位不声不响的人在花园里侍弄。同埃尔德里克和拜纳谈话的前一天傍晚，科林伍德就是这样瞧见他的——正忙着往花坛里下花籽。于是他问了问科布克罗夫特太太——她那会儿正在他的起居室里——她先生是不是喜好园艺。

===Original===
“It’s a nice change for him, sir,” answered the landlady. “He’s kept pretty close at it all day in the office yonder at Mallathorpe’s Mill, and it does him good to get a bit o’ fresh air at nights, now that the fine weather’s coming on. That was one reason why we took this old place—it’s a deal better air here nor what it is in the town.”

===Chinese===
“这对他是份挺好的调剂，先生，”房东太太答道。“他在那边马拉索普工厂的账房里，成天价关着做事，眼下好天气一天天来了，晚上透点新鲜空气，对他身子有益。俺们当初要下这所老宅子，这也是一个缘故——这儿的空气，可比城里强得多哩。”

===Original===
“So your husband is at Mallathorpe’s Mill, eh?” asked Collingwood.

===Chinese===
“这么说，您先生是在马拉索普工厂做事喽？”科林伍德问。

===Original===
“Been there—in the countinghouse—boy and man, over thirty years, sir,” replied Mrs. Cobcroft.

===Chinese===
“在那儿——在账房里——打从做小伙计到如今，三十多年了，先生，”科布克罗夫特太太答道。

===Original===
“Did he see that terrible affair then—was it two years ago?”

===Chinese===
“那场可怕的祸事，他可是亲眼瞧见了？——是两年前的事吧？”

===Original===
The landlady shook her head and let out a weighty sigh.

===Chinese===
房东太太摇了摇头，长长地叹出一口沉重的气。

===Original===
“Aye, I should think he did!” she answered. “And a nice shock it gave him, too!—he actually saw that chimney fall—him and another clerk were looking out o’ the countinghouse window when it gave way.”

===Chinese===
“哎，可不是瞧见了嘛！”她答道。“还叫他吃了好大一场惊吓呢！——那根烟囱倒下来，他是亲眼看见的——塌的时候，他同账房里另一个职员正从窗户里往外望呢。”

===Original===
Collingwood said no more then—except to remark that such a sight must indeed have been trying to the nerves. But for purposes of his own he determined to have a talk with Cobcroft, and the next evening, seeing him in his garden again, he went out to him and got into conversation, and eventually led up to the subject of Mallathorpe’s Mill, the new chimney of which could be seen from a corner of the garden.

===Chinese===
科林伍德当时没再多说——只道了句，那样的场面，想必实在叫人神经受不住。不过他自有打算，决意要同科布克罗夫特谈一谈。第二天傍晚，又瞧见他在花园里，他便踱出去同他搭上话，渐渐引到马拉索普工厂的话题上——从花园的一个角落里，正好望得见厂里那根新烟囱。

===Original===
“Your wife tells me,” observed Collingwood, “that you were present when the old chimney fell at the mill yonder?”

===Chinese===
“您太太告诉我，”科林伍德说，“那边厂里老烟囱倒塌的时候，您正在场？”

===Original===
Cobcroft, a quiet, unassuming man, usually of few words, looked along the hillside at the new chimney, and nodded his head. A curious, faraway look came into his eyes.

===Chinese===
科布克罗夫特是个安静谦和的人，平素寡言少语。他顺着山坡望了望那根新烟囱，点了点头。他眼睛里浮起一种悠远而古怪的神色。

===Original===
“I was, sir!” he said. “And I hope I may never see aught o’ that sort again, as long as ever I live. It was one o’ those things a man can never forget!”

===Chinese===
“俺是在场，先生！”他说。“但愿俺这一辈子，再别瞧见那样的事。那是人一辈子也忘不掉的一号事啊！”

===Original===
“Don’t talk about it if you don’t want to,” remarked Collingwood. “But I’ve heard so much about that affair that—”

===Chinese===
“您要是不愿意讲，就不必讲了，”科林伍德说。“只是这桩事我听人讲了那么多，所以——”

===Original===
“Oh, I don’t mind talking about it,” replied Cobcroft. He leaned over the fence of his garden, still gazing at the mill in the distance. “There were others that saw it, of course: lots of ’em. But I was close at hand—our office was filled with the dust in a few seconds.”

===Chinese===
“哦，讲讲倒不打紧，”科布克罗夫特答道。他俯在花园的栅栏上，眼睛仍旧望着远处那座工厂。“瞧见的自然不止俺一个：多着呢。可俺离得近——没几秒钟，俺们账房里就灌满了那阵灰土。”

===Original===
“It was a sudden affair?” asked Collingwood.

===Chinese===
“事情来得很突然？”科林伍德问。

===Original===
“It was one of those affairs,” answered Cobcroft slowly, “that some folk had been expecting for a long time—only nobody had the sense to see that it might happen at some unexpected minute. It was a very old chimney. It looked all right—stood plumb, and all that. But Mr. Mallathorpe—my old master, Mr. John Mallathorpe, I’m talking of—he got an idea from two or three little things, d’ye see, that it wasn’t as safe as it ought to be. And he got a couple of these professional steeplejacks to examine it. They made a thorough examination, too—so far as one could tell by what they did. They’d been at the job several days when the accident happened. One of ’em had only just come down when the chimney fell. Mr. Mallathorpe, himself, and his manager, and his cashier, had just stepped out of the countinghouse and crossed the yard to hear what this man had got to say when—down it came! Not the slightest warning at the time. It just—collapsed!”

===Chinese===
“那号事情啊，”科布克罗夫特慢吞吞地答道，“有些人早就在心里料着了——只是谁也没那份心眼，想到它兴许会在哪个没提防的时辰上出岔子。那是根很老很老的烟囱。瞧着好好的——立得笔直，诸如此类。可是马拉索普先生——俺说的是俺的老东家，约翰·马拉索普先生——从两三件小事上头起了疑心，您明白吗，觉着它不像它该有的那么牢靠。于是他请了两个吃这行饭的尖塔检修工来查验。他们查得也着实仔细——至少从他们手底的做派上瞧是如此。出事的时候，这活计他们已经干了几天了。烟囱倒的时候，其中一个才刚从上头下来。马拉索普先生本人，连同他的经理长、他的出纳主任，刚跨出账房，穿过场院，正要去听那人回话——就在这当口，轰隆一声塌了下来！事先没有半点预警。它就是——整个儿垮塌了！”

===Original===
“You saw the actual collapse?” asked Collingwood.

===Chinese===
“倒塌的实况，您亲眼瞧见了？”科林伍德问。

===Original===
“Aye—didn’t I?” exclaimed Cobcroft. “Another man and myself were looking out of the office window, right opposite. It fell in the queerest way—like this,” he went on, holding up his garden-rake. “Supposing this shaft was the chimney—standing straight up. As we looked we saw it suddenly bulge out, on all sides—it was a square chimney, same size all the way up till you got to the cornice at the top—bulge out, d’ye see, just about halfway up—simultaneous, like. Then—down it came with a roar that they heard over half the town! O’ course, there were some two or three thousands of tons of stuff in that chimney—and when the dust was cleared a bit there it was in one great heap, right across the yard. And it was a good job,” concluded Cobcroft, reflectively, “that it fell straight—collapsed in itself, as you might say—for if it had fallen slanting either way, it ’ud ha’ smashed right through some of the sheds, and there’d ha’ been a terrible loss of life.”

===Chinese===
“哎——可不就是嘛！”科布克罗夫特高声说。“另一个人同俺，正对着面从账房窗户往外瞧。它倒下来的那个样子，才叫古怪呢——就像这样，”他说着，举起手里那把园圃钉耙。“就算这根耙柄是那根烟囱——直挺挺竖着。俺们正瞧着，忽见它四面向外一鼓——那是根方烟囱，一路上去一般粗细，直到顶上的檐口——您瞧，就在半腰上，四面同时鼓了出去——就像听着一个口令一齐使劲似的。跟着——轰隆一声倒了下来，半个镇子都听见了！当然啦，那烟囱里头总有两三千吨的物料——等灰土稍微落定一点，好家伙，一大堆正正地横贯了整个场院。还算万幸，”科布克罗夫特沉吟着收梢道，“它倒得笔直——可以说是照着自个儿的身子塌下去的——它要是往哪一边歪着倒，准把好几处棚屋砸穿，那可就要有许多人送命了。”

===Original===
“Mr. John Mallathorpe was killed on the spot, I believe?” suggested Collingwood.

===Chinese===
“约翰·马拉索普先生是当场罹难的吧？”科林伍德提了一句。

===Original===
“Aye—and Gaukrodger, and Marshall, and the steeplejack that had just come down, and another or two,” said Cobcroft. “They’d no chance—they were standing in a group at the very foot, talking. They were all killed there and then—instantaneous. Some others were struck and injured—one or two died. Yes, sir—I’m not very like to forget that!”

===Chinese===
“哎——还有高克罗杰，还有马歇尔，还有那个刚下来的尖塔检修工，另外还有一两个，”科布克罗夫特说。“他们一点逃躲的份儿都没有——几个人正扎堆站在烟囱根脚下说话呢。就在那儿，当时当下，全都死了——一眨眼的事。另有几个人叫砸着受了伤——死了一两个。是呀，先生——这个俺怕是忘不了啦！”

===Original===
“A terrible experience!” agreed Collingwood. “It would naturally fix itself on your memory.”

===Chinese===
“真是可怕的经历！”科林伍德深有同感。“那样的场面，自然会牢牢刻在您的记忆里。”

===Original===
“Aye—my memory’s very keen about it,” said Cobcroft. “I remember every detail of that morning. And,” he continued, showing a desire to become reminiscent, “there was something happened that morning, before the accident, that I’ve oft thought over and has oft puzzled me. I’ve never said aught to anybody about it, because we Yorkshiremen we’re not given to talking about affairs that don’t concern us, and after all, it was none o’ mine! But you’re a law gentleman, and I dare say you get things told to you in confidence now and then, and, of course, this is between you and me. I’ll not deny that I have oft thought that I would like to tell it to a lawyer of some sort, and find out how it struck him.”

===Chinese===
“哎——对那一节，俺的记性清楚得很，”科布克罗夫特说。“那天早上的一情一节，俺全记得。再说，”他接着往下讲，露出想要絮叨旧事的模样，“那天早上，出事之前，还发生过一件事，俺后来常琢磨，也常纳闷。俺从来没跟任何人提过半个字，因为俺们约克郡人，不兴多嘴去讲不干自己的事，说到底，那也不干俺的事！可您是位法律先生，俺想您少不了时常有人私下里把体己话讲给您听，当然啦，这话只出在您我二人之间。俺不否认，俺不止一回想过，要把这桩事讲给个把律师听听，瞧瞧他心里怎么个看法。”

===Original===
“Anything that you like to tell me, Mr. Cobcroft, I shall treat as a matter of confidence—until you tell me it’s no longer a secret,” answered Collingwood.

===Chinese===
“您愿意讲给我听的任何事情，科布克罗夫特先生，我都当作机密之事对待——直到您亲口对我说，它不再是秘密为止，”科林伍德答道。

===Original===
